| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_CONVERSATION_TRACKING`                   | _(ba a saita ba)_                | `open-sse/services/conversationTracker.ts` | Saita zuwa `1` don dakatar da tattara tarihin tattaunawa. `resolveConversationId()` yana mayar da sakamakon da ba a sa ido a kansa kafin ya karanta SQLite ko ya tantance tarihin saƙonni, kuma wannan ya haɗa da ID ɗin zaman da abokin ciniki ya bayar. Gudanar da zaman turawa bai canza ba, kuma ba a share bayanan da suke akwai. Wannan ya dace da girke-girken da ba sa amfani da kallon tattaunawa na dashboard kuma suke son hana jadawalin zagaye ci gaba da girma.                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_WEEKLY_WINDOW`                           | `provider`                       | `src/lib/usage/apiKeyUsageLimits.ts`       | Tagar mako don iyakokin amfani da USD ga kowane maɓallin API (tilasta iyaka ga buƙatu, `GET /api/keys/{id}/usage-limits`, `@@om-usage`). `provider`: makon yana ƙarewa a lokacin sake saitin mako mafi kusa cikin haɗin sabis na sama da maɓallin zai iya isa gare su, ko kuma yana zama zangon kwanaki 7 mai zamewa idan ba a san wani lokacin sake saiti ba. `calendar`: makon gida daga Litinin 00:00 zuwa Litinin 00:00 ta gaba, iri ɗaya ga kowane maɓalli. Ana ba da shawarar `calendar` idan maɓallai da yawa, kowannensu da kasafin kuɗinsa, suna raba rukunin asusun sabis na sama guda ɗaya. Ƙimomin da ba su inganta ba suna komawa zuwa `provider`. |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_WEEKLY_WINDOW_TIMEZONE`                  | _(yankin lokacin aikin shirin)_  | `src/lib/usage/apiKeyUsageLimits.ts`       | Yankin lokacin IANA (misali `Asia/Shanghai`) wanda yake ƙayyade Litinin 00:00 don tagar mako ta `calendar`. Idan ba a saita shi ba ko bai inganta ba: yankin lokacin aikin shirin OmniRoute (wato `TZ` na na'urar masauki ko kwantena, wanda sau da yawa ba a saita shi ba a hotunan kwantena, wato Lokacin Duniya Mai Daidaituwa). Ba a amfani da shi a yanayin `provider`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |

## 7. URLs & Aiki Tare da Cloud

| Variable                                 | Default                                         | Source File                                      | Description                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| ---------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `BASE_URL`                               | `http://localhost:20128`                        | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                           | URL na gefen sabar da ayyukan aiki tare na ciki za su kira `/api/sync/cloud` da shi. A bar wannan a matsayin URL na loopback/container ko da ana wakiltar manhajar ga jama'a ta hanyar proxy.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `CLOUD_URL`                              | _(babu komai)_                                  | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                           | URL na endpoint na isar da bayanai ta cloud (fasalin premium).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `CLOUD_SYNC_TIMEOUT_MS`                  | `12000`                                         | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                           | Iyakar lokacin HTTP don buƙatun aiki tare da cloud.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_BUILD_PROFILE`                | `full`                                          | Saitin build na Webpack                          | Bayanan martabar lokacin build (saita zuwa `minimal` don cire modules masu gata daga bundle gaba ɗaya).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_STANDALONE_DIR`               | _fitowar standalone ta .build/_                 | `scripts/build/colocate-standalone.mjs`          | Sauya saitin lokacin build don kundin adireshin fitowar standalone wanda matakin colocation na bayan build ke amfani da shi. Wannan ba saitin runtime ba ne.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLOUD_SYNC_SECRET`            | _(babu komai)_                                  | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                           | Sirrin da aka raba wanda ake amfani da shi don tabbatar da sa hannun HMAC-SHA256 na martanin Cloud Sync.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLOUD_SYNC_SECRETS`           | `false`                                         | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                           | Saita zuwa `true` don bai wa endpoint na Cloud Sync damar maye gurbin bayanan shaidar gida. Tsohon saitin shi ne `false`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |

Duk sa hannun da yake akwai ana tabbatar da shi koyaushe — kuma koyaushe ana ƙin karɓarsa idan ba a saita `OMNIROUTE_CLOUD_SYNC_SECRET` ba — ba tare da la'akari da wannan tuta ba. A v3.9, tsohon saitin zai koma tilasta wannan.                                                                                                                                                       |

Sauya shi don relays / kayan gwaji.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |

Mai matuƙar muhimmanci; fi son filin Dashboard na kowane haɗi idan an saita asusu da yawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |

Mai matuƙar muhimmanci; fi son filin Dashboard na kowane haɗi idan an saita asusu da yawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEWHISPERER_BASE_URL`       | `https://codewhisperer.us-east-1.amazonaws.com` | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                     | Endpoint na iyakokin amfani da CodeWhisperer (AWS Kiro). Sauya shi don relays / kayan gwaji.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |

> [!IMPORTANT]
> Lokacin turawa a bayan reverse proxy (nginx, Caddy), saita `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL` zuwa tabbataccen URL ɗin jama'a naka (misali, `https://omniroute.example.com`) idan OAuth callbacks ko hanyoyin jama'a da aka ƙirƙira dole ne su yi amfani da wannan hostname. Idan babu wannan, OAuth callbacks na iya gaza saboda redirect_uri ba zai dace ba, kuma hanyoyin jama'a da aka ƙirƙira za su iya nuna asalin container na ciki.
>
> Bar `BASE_URL` a matsayin URL na loopback/container na ciki don ayyukan server-to-server. Kada a yi amfani da `Origin` na browser ko hostname na jama'a don internal self-fetches masu ɗauke da bayanan shiga.
>
> Rubuce-rubucen dashboard da aka tantance ba sa buƙatar tsayayyen public base URL: dashboard yana aika same-origin unsafe requests tare da session-bound CSRF token. Har yanzu OmniRoute yana tattara tantance public-origin a wuri guda don browser integrations waɗanda ba na dashboard ba: ana fara amincewa da bayyanannun public URL env vars; ana yin watsi da ɗanyen taken `Forwarded` / `X-Forwarded-*` sai dai idan an kunna `OMNIROUTE_TRUST_PROXY` kuma an yi wa immediate proxy peer alamar token a matsayin abin amincewa. Kada a yi amfani da saitunan CORS don gyara same-origin dashboard requests; CORS na cross-origin browser clients ne kawai.

---

## 8. Wakilin Hanyar Fita

Tura kiraye-kirayen mai samar da LLM na waje ta hanyar wakilin HTTP ko SOCKS5 don sarrafa zirga-zirgar fita, karkatar da zirga-zirga bisa yanki, ko ɓoye adireshin IP.

`OMNIROUTE_UPSTREAM_HTTP2_ENABLED` yana amfani da `true` ta tsohuwa. Saita shi zuwa `false`, `0`, `no`, ko
`off` don tilasta HTTP/1.1 a kan masu aika buƙata na Undici da ke cikin `open-sse/utils/proxyDispatcher.ts`
da `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`: fita kai tsaye, wakilan HTTP(S)/SOCKS, masu isarwa, da
sake gwadawarsu ta sabbin soket. Wannan yana kiyaye tabbatar da takardar shaidar TLS, tantancewar
wakili, iyakokin haɗi, da saitunan keep-alive. Sake kunna OmniRoute bayan
canza shi, saboda ana adana masu aika buƙata a ma'ajiyar wucin gadi kuma ana ƙirƙirar wakilan isarwa lokacin farawa.
Wannan sauyin ba ya sarrafa kwaikwayon sawun TLS na wreq ko hanyoyin jigilar bayanai na musamman ga masu samarwa.

| Sauyawa                                  | Tsoho             | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| ---------------------------------------- | ----------------- | ---------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `ENABLE_SOCKS5_PROXY`                    | `true`            | `open-sse/executors`                           | Kunna wakilin SOCKS5 proxy don kiraye-kirayen zuwa sama. Kashe shi da `false`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_ENABLE_SOCKS5_PROXY`        | `true`            | Bangaren abokin hulɗa                          | Fahimtar kasancewar SOCKS5 a ɓangaren abokin hulɗa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `PROXY_SKIP_RECENTLY_FAILED`             | `true`            | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`             | Tutar fasali da take kunne ta tsohuwa (duba [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md); sauya ƙimar DB na dashboard yana da fifiko). Tarin proxy da juyawa na kowane asusu suna daina sake ba da memban da ya gaza kwanan nan (gwajin TCP da aka ƙi, ko 429 ta cikinsa) na wani lokaci da ke ninkuwa a kowane maimaituwa, har zuwa iyaka. `false` yana dawo da zaɓi na yau da kullum.                                                                                                           |
| `SELECTOR_CONTROL_ALLOWLIST`             | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `src/lib/proxySubscription/selectorGuard.ts`   | Jerin izini na maƙasudan sarrafa-mai-zaɓi na local-core bayan loopback (hosts ko IPs da aka raba da wakafi/sarari, `*` yana buɗe LAN/private amma ba metadata/link-local ba). Ana ba da izinin loopback ta http/https ta tsohuwa. Ƙimar DB na dashboard tana da fifiko kan wannan sauyawa idan an saita ta kuma ba fanko ba ce.                                                                                                                                                             |
| `PROXY_QUOTA_429_BASE_MS`                | `300000`          | `open-sse/utils/proxyRefusalMemory.ts`         | Tsawon lokaci na asali (ms) na warewar 429, wanda ake ninkawa a kowane maimaituwa. Ana karanta shi sau ɗaya a lokacin farawa. An iyakance shi zuwa `1000..3600000`; ƙimar da ta fita daga iyaka ko wadda ba za a iya karantawa ba tana komawa zuwa tsohuwar ƙima tare da gargaɗi.                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `PROXY_QUOTA_429_MAX_MS`                 | `900000`          | `open-sse/utils/proxyRefusalMemory.ts`         | Iyakar (ms) warewar 429. Ana karanta ta sau ɗaya a lokacin farawa. An iyakance ta zuwa `1000..3600000`; ƙimar da ta fita daga iyaka ko wadda ba za a iya karantawa ba tana komawa zuwa tsohuwar ƙima tare da gargaɗi. Idan iyakar ta yi ƙasa da ƙimar asali, tana komawa zuwa tsohuwar ƙima. Rage iyakar kuma yana taƙaita manta jerin gazawa: ana share shigarwa `2 x max` bayan ƙarewar wa'adi.                                                                                           |
| `PROXY_POOL_SHARED_EGRESS_ORDER`         | `false`           | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`             | Tutar fasalin da ake kunna ta bisa zaɓi (duba [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md); sauya ƙimar DB na dashboard yana da fifiko). Ga masu samarwa waɗanda ake rarraba ƙididdigar amfaninsu bisa adireshin egress, a jera memban tari da yake amfani da adireshin egress ɗaya da na memban da aka ƙi kwanan nan a ƙasa kaɗan da mambobi masu lafiya. Tsarawa kawai ake yi, ba a taɓa cirewa ba. Yana buƙatar `PROXY_SKIP_RECENTLY_FAILED`, wanda ke samar da siginar ƙin da yake karantawa. |
| `PROXY_WEBHOOK_REBOUND_MS`               | `300000`          | `src/lib/proxyEvents/proxyTransitionBridge.ts` | Tagar rebound (ms) da ke iyakance maimaita faɗakarwar webhook ta `proxy.set_aside` ga kowane memban tari domin tarin ƙin yarda ya aika da abu guda kawai. Ana karanta ta a kowane fitarwa. An iyakance ta zuwa `1000..3600000`; ƙimar da ta fita daga iyaka ko wadda ba za a iya karantawa ba tana komawa zuwa tsohuwar ƙima ba tare da gargaɗi ba.                                                                                                                                         |
| `HTTP_PROXY`                             | _(ba a saita ba)_ | Matsayin Node.js                               | HTTP proxy don kiraye-kirayen zuwa sama.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `HTTPS_PROXY`                            | _(ba a saita ba)_ | Matsayin Node.js                               | HTTPS proxy don kiraye-kirayen zuwa sama.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `ALL_PROXY`                              | _(ba a saita ba)_ | Matsayin Node.js                               | Proxy na bai ɗaya (yana goyon bayan `socks5://`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_ECHO_URL`               | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `src/lib/proxyEchoTarget.ts`                   | Yana kayyade maƙasudin echo-IP da binciken fitawar proxy ke amfani da shi zuwa URL guda ɗaya. Idan ba a saita ba, binciken zai fara gwada `api64.ipify.org`, sannan `api4.ipify.org`, don kada a ba da rahoton cewa tunnels masu IPv4 kaɗai sun daina aiki (#9694).                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `NO_PROXY`                               | _(ba a saita ba)_ | Ma'aunin Node.js                               | Sunayen hosts/IPs da aka raba da waƙafi waɗanda za a tsallake proxy dominsu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_DISPATCHER_CONNECTIONS` | `32`              | `open-sse/utils/proxyDispatcher.ts`            | Matsakaicin sockets masu aiki a lokaci guda ga kowane HTTP/SOCKS proxy dispatcher da aka adana a cache. Dogayen rafukan SSE kamar Codex `/v1/responses` suna buƙatar haɗi fiye da ɗaya idan buƙatu da yawa suna amfani da proxy ɗaya na matakin asusu. Ana iyakance ƙimomin da suka wuce `256`.                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `SOCKS_HANDSHAKE_TIMEOUT_MS`             | `10000`           | `open-sse/utils/socksConnectorWithFamily.ts`   | Lokacin ƙarewar SOCKS5 handshake (connect) a ms. Ƙara shi idan buƙatu masu yawa a lokaci guda suka bugi host guda na residential gateway (misali, buƙatu 100 a lokaci guda) — ainihin handshake na iya wuce 10s idan pool ya cika, ko da yake ana iya isa ga proxy, wanda in ba haka ba zai bayyana a matsayin kuskuren ƙarya na `[Proxy Fast-Fail] Ba za a iya isa ga proxy ba`. An iyakance shi zuwa `120000`.                                                                            |
| `PROXY_FAIL_OPEN`                        | `false`           | `src/sse/handlers/chatHelpers.ts`              | Idan `false` (tsoho), buƙatar da proxy ɗin da aka ware mata ya kasa warwarewa za a **ƙi karɓa (fail-closed)** maimakon komawa ga haɗin kai tsaye — wannan yana hana zubewar real-IP. Saita `true` don dawo da tsohon DIRECT fallback.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `ENABLE_TLS_FINGERPRINT`                 | `false`           | `open-sse/executors`                           | Kwaikwayi TLS fingerprint ta amfani da wreq-js (yana kwaikwayon Chrome 124). Yana magance toshewar JA3/JA4.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `TLS_FINGERPRINT_PROVIDERS`              | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`                 | Jerin providers da aka yarda da su, wanda aka raba da waƙafi, don sabon proxied TLS routing (`open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`). Direct TLS yana ci gaba da tsohon halinsa idan ba a saita ba; waɗannan providers kaɗai ne za su bi ta Chrome-124 fingerprint bridge.                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_TURNSTILE_IGNORE_TLS_ERRORS`  | `false`           | `open-sse/services/claudeTurnstileSolver.ts`   | Ba mahallin burauzar Claude Turnstile Playwright damar yin watsi da kurakuran takardar shaidar HTTPS.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |

### Yanayin Amfani

| Yanayi                                               | Kanfigareshan                                                                                                                        |
| ---------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| **SOCKS5 ta SSH tunnel**                             | `ALL_PROXY=socks5://127.0.0.1:7890`, `ENABLE_SOCKS5_PROXY=true`                                                                      |
| **HTTP proxy na kamfani**                            | `HTTP_PROXY=http://proxy.corp.com:3128`, `HTTPS_PROXY=http://proxy.corp.com:3128`, `NO_PROXY=localhost,internal.corp.com`            |
| **Karewa daga fingerprinting**                       | `ENABLE_TLS_FINGERPRINT=true` — yana buƙatar `wreq-js` (an haɗa shi)                                                                 |
| **Ana sarrafa fitarwa / babu damar shiga kai tsaye** | Bar `PROXY_FAIL_OPEN=false` (tsoho). Buƙatu za su gaza gaba ɗaya idan babu proxy maimakon fallasa bayanai ta hanyar haɗin kai tsaye. |
| **Tsohon tsari / dev — ba da damar direct fallback** | `PROXY_FAIL_OPEN=true`. Yana dawo da halin kafin ƙarfafa tsaro: ana amfani da haɗin kai tsaye idan warware proxy ya gaza.            |

> **Lura (tsallake tantancewar NVIDIA — #3226):** Wurin ƙarshen tantance API-key na NVIDIA
> yana maƙalewa idan aka bi da shi ta global proxy/TLS-patched fetch (undici dispatcher → 504).
> `src/lib/providers/validation.ts::directHttpsRequest()` da gangan yana tsallake
> proxy patch don wannan kiran tantancewa guda ɗaya ta amfani da `safeOutboundFetch({ bypassProxyPatch: true })`.
> Wannan keɓantaccen yanayi ne da aka rubuta kuma aka iyakance — **ba ya** shafar fitawar chat/usage.
> An kayyade iyakar tsallakewar ta `tests/unit/proxy-bypass-scope-guard-3226.test.ts`.

---

## 9. Haɗa Kayan Aikin CLI

Yana sarrafa yadda OmniRoute ke ganowa da ƙaddamar da sidecars na CLI (Claude Code, Codex, da sauransu).

| Maɓalli                           | Tsoho                            | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                     | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| --------------------------------- | -------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CLI_MODE`                        | `auto`                           | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | `auto` = bincika PATH na tsarin; `manual` = yi amfani da takamaiman hanyoyi kawai.                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CLI_EXTRA_PATHS`                 | _(ba a saita ba)_                | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ƙarin shigarwar PATH don gano fayilolin binary na CLI (an raba su da alamar wakafi mai digo).                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `CLI_CONFIG_HOME`                 | _(ba a saita ba)_                | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Sauya kundin gida don karanta saitunan CLI (`~/.claude`, `~/.codex`). Dole ne ya zama cikakkiyar hanya kuma yana cikin gidan tsarin aiki — **ko kuma**, a cikin container, hanyar da aka bind-mount (haka `/host-home` ke aiki). Duk wani abu dabam zai koma kundin gida. |
| `CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES`         | `true`                           | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ba OmniRoute damar rubuta fayilolin saitin CLI (sabunta token, bayanan zama). Saita zuwa `false` domin duk wani yunƙurin rubuta saitin CLI ya gaza tare da takamaiman kuskuren "an kashe rubutawa".                                                                       |
| `CLI_CLAUDE_BIN`                  | `claude`                         | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Hanya ta musamman zuwa fayil ɗin binary na Claude CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CLI_CODEX_BIN`                   | `codex`                          | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Hanya ta musamman zuwa fayil ɗin binary na Codex CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |

Ya yi daidai da env var da mai girka Hermes PowerShell ke saitawa a Windows (`%LOCALAPPDATA%\hermes`).                                                                                  |

### Daidaita Bayanan Martabar CLI ta Atomatik

Waɗannan tutocin fasali na zaɓi ne kuma a kashe suke ta asali. Haka kuma, ana iya kunna ko kashe su daga
dashboard na CLI Code.

| Maɓalli                               | Na Asali | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                  | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| ------------------------------------- | -------- | ------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_AUTO_SYNC_CODEX_PROFILES`  | `false`  | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts` | Bayan daidaita samfurin mai samarwa, yana sake rubuta fayilolin bayanan martaba na `~/.codex/*.config.toml` ta atomatik daga kundin bayanai mai aiki. Yana buƙatar `CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES`; ba ya taɓa canza saitin Codex mai aiki/na asali, tantancewa, saitunan Codex-lb, ko zaɓin mai samarwa.               |
| `OMNIROUTE_AUTO_SYNC_CLAUDE_PROFILES` | `false`  | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts` | Bayan daidaita samfurin mai samarwa, yana sake rubuta fayilolin bayanan martaba na Claude Code na `~/.claude/profiles/<name>/settings.json` ta atomatik daga kundin bayanai mai aiki. Yana buƙatar `CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES`; ba ya taɓa canza saitin Claude mai aiki/na asali, tantancewa, ko zaɓin mai samarwa. |

### Misalin Docker

```bash
# Haɗa binary na na'urar masauki cikin container sannan ka gaya wa OmniRoute inda suke:
CLI_EXTRA_PATHS=/host-cli/bin
CLI_CONFIG_HOME=/host-home
CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES=true
CLI_CLAUDE_BIN=/host-cli/bin/claude
```

`CLI_CONFIG_HOME` yana fara aiki ne kawai idan an ɗaura hanyar a matsayin bind mount daga
na'urar mai masaukin baki — haɗa shi da mounts kamar `~/.codex:/host-home/.codex:rw` (duba
profile na `host` a cikin `docker-compose.yml`). Ana yin watsi da hanyar da ba ta cikin
home na mai amfani da container kuma ba bind mount ba ce, saboda duk abin da aka rubuta a wurin
zai ɓace idan aka sake ƙirƙirar container ɗin.

Image ɗin yana gudana a matsayin `USER node`, don haka `/root` da ba a yi masa mount ba **ba**
ingantaccen override ba ne.

| Variable                                 | Default         | Source File                         | Bayani                                                                                                                                                          |
| ---------------------------------------- | --------------- | ----------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTAINER`                    | _(ta atomatik)_ | `src/shared/utils/containerEnv.ts`  | Tilasta gano container ya kasance a kunne (`1`/`true`) ko a kashe (`0`/`false`). Ana buƙatar wannan ne kawai a runtimes da ganowar ta atomatik ba ta gano ba.   |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_CONTAINER_CONFIG_WRITE` | `false`         | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts` | Ba da damar rubuta saitunan kayan aikin CLI cikin hanyar container da ba a yi wa mount ba duk da haka. Daidaitaccen zaɓin CLI shi ne `--allow-container-write`. |

### Mataimakan Binary na CLI (`omniroute`)

Waɗannan variables suna daidaita halayen binary na CLI na `omniroute` kansa (ba ganowar sidecar
da ke sama ba).

| Variable                              | Default           | Source File                             | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ------------------------------------- | ----------------- | --------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_LANG`                      | _(system)_        | `bin/cli/i18n.mjs`                      | Tilasta harshen output na CLI. Locale na BCP-47 (misali `en`, `pt-BR`). Yana override variables na locale na system (LC_ALL, LC_MESSAGES).                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_SHOW_LOG`                  | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `bin/cli/runtime/processSupervisor.mjs` | Saita zuwa `1` don tura stdout/stderr na server zuwa terminal a supervised mode. Daidai yake da flag na `--log` a kan `omniroute serve`.                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLI_TOKEN`                 | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `bin/cli/api.mjs`                       | Token na tabbatar da na'ura da ake saka a matsayin header na `x-omniroute-cli-token`. Ana samar da shi ta atomatik a task 8.12.                                                                                                                                           |
| `GEMINI_CLI_HOME`                     | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `bin/cli/commands/setup-gemini.mjs`     | Tushen home na Gemini mai ɗorewa; gudanarwa ta sau ɗaya tana amfani da home na wucin gadi da aka ware.                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHILD_SIGNAL_TIMEOUT_MS`   | `5000`            | `bin/cli/commands/run.mjs`              | Lokacin jinkiri a milliseconds kafin a kashe child na CLI da aka aika wa signal da ƙarfi.                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLI_SKIP_DEFAULT_DATA_ENV` | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `bin/omniroute.mjs`                     | Saita zuwa 1 tare da DATA_DIR da aka fayyace don tsallake environment file na data-directory na tsoho mai amfani.                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_HTTP_TIMEOUT_MS`           | `30000`           | `bin/cli/api.mjs`                       | Iyakar lokacin HTTP na kowace gwaji (ms) don buƙatun CLI → server.                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_READY_TIMEOUT_MS`          | `60000`           | `bin/cli/utils/pid.mjs`                 | Matsakaicin lokacin (ms) da CLI ke jira endpoint na lafiyar server kafin ya buga gargaɗin ƙarewar lokaci. Yana da amfani ga cold starts masu jinkiri (misali Windows). Hakanan ana iya saita shi ta `--ready-timeout`.                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_VERBOSE`                   | `0`               | `bin/cli/api.mjs`                       | Saita zuwa `1` don buga bayanan bincike na retry/backoff zuwa stderr yayin umarnin CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_PLUGIN_PATH`               | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `bin/cli/plugins.mjs`                   | Directory na musamman don gano plugins na CLI (packages na `omniroute-cmd-*`). Yana komawa zuwa `~/.omniroute/plugins/` idan ba a saita ba. Na CLI kawai — ba ya taɓa isa ga scanner na plugins na ɓangaren server, wanda `OMNIROUTE_PLUGINS_DIR` ke nunawa (sashe na 2). |

---

## 10. Haɗaɗɗun Wakilin Ciki & MCP

| Sauƙaƙe                                         | Tsoho                                               | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                             | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| ----------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_BASE_URL`                            | ganowa ta atomatik                                  | `open-sse/mcp-server/server.ts`                             | URL da aka fayyace domin kayan aikin MCP/A2A su isa OmniRoute. Yana soke ganowa ta atomatik ta localhost.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY`                             | _(ba a saita ba)_                                   | Modulan MCP/A2A                                             | Maɓallin API don kiran kayan aikin MCP na ciki da ƙwarewar A2A.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_ID`                          | _(ba a saita ba)_                                   | `open-sse/mcp-server/audit.ts`                              | ID na maɓalli don danganta rajistan binciken MCP.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `ROUTER_API_KEY`                                | _(ba a saita ba)_                                   | Na gado                                                     | Tsohon laƙabi na `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_A2A_HISTORY_RETENTION_DAYS`          | `30`                                                | `src/lib/a2a/taskManager.ts`                                | Adadin kwanakin tarihin ayyukan A2A da ake ajiyewa a ma'ajiyar bayanai ta gida kafin sharewar yau da kullum ta goge layi. Idan ba a saita ba, ba lamba ba ne, ko `<= 0`, zai koma `30`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_A2A_MEMORY_HITS`                     | `1`                                                 | `src/lib/a2a/taskExecution.ts`                              | Maɓallin kashe fasalin lura da buguwar ƙwaƙwalwar A2A. Saita zuwa `0` don tsallake binciken dawo da ƙwaƙwalwa na wani aiki gaba ɗaya; kowace ƙima daban (har da rashin saitawa) tana barin fasalin a kunne.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_ISSUE_AGENT_ENABLED`                 | `false`                                             | `src/app/api/issue-agent/runs/route.ts`                     | Yana kunna endpoint na Issue Agent na offline/na gida don tantance matsalolin da aka yi rikodi. Bar shi a kashe sai idan ana gudanar da hanyoyin aiki na gida na tantance rikodi a sarari.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_ISSUE_AGENT_TIMEOUT_MS`              | _(ba a saita ba)_                                   | `src/lib/issueAgent/execution.ts`                           | Iyakar lokaci (ms) na gudu guda na tantance rikodi na Issue Agent. Ana iyakance shi zuwa matsakaicin ciki; yana komawa tsohon ƙimar da aka gina idan ba a saita shi ba ko ba shi da inganci.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTEXT`                             | _(mahallin da ke aiki)_                             | `bin/cli/program.mjs`, `bin/cli/api.mjs`                    | Mahalli/furofayil na yanayin nesa na CLI don umarnin `omniroute`; yana soke mahallin da ke aiki a ma'ajiyar mahallai ta gida. Daidai yake da `--context <name>`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTEXT_KEYCHAIN_DISABLED`           | `0`                                                 | `bin/cli/contexts.mjs`                                      | Kashe backend na keychain na OS na `keytar` wanda zaɓi ne don bayanan shaidar mahallin CLI. Idan an kunna, bayanan shaidar za su ci gaba da kasancewa a yanayin `config.json` `0600`, kuma CLI zai fitar da gargaɗin komawa madadin sau ɗaya; an yi niyya don aiki da gangan ba tare da allon sarrafawa ba/a cikin container.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_ENFORCE_SCOPES`                  | `false`                                             | `open-sse/mcp-server/server.ts`                             | Tilasta sarrafa samun dama bisa scope a kan kiran kayan aikin MCP.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_SCOPES`                          | _(duka)_                                            | `open-sse/mcp-server/server.ts`                             | Scopes da waƙafi ya raba: `admin`, `combos`, `health`, `models`, `routing`, `budget`, `metrics`, `pricing`, `memory`, `skills`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_COMPRESS_DESCRIPTIONS`           | `false`                                             | `open-sse/mcp-server/descriptionCompressor.ts`              | Matse bayanan kayan aikin MCP kafin jerantar da manifest. Ƙimomin kunnawa: `1`, `true`, `on`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_DESCRIPTION_COMPRESSION`         | `rtk`                                               | `open-sse/mcp-server/descriptionCompressor.ts`              | Algoritim/furofayil na matsewa. Ƙimomin kashewa: `0`, `false`, `off`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                | `10000`                                             | `open-sse/mcp-server/fetchTimeout.ts`                       | Kasafin lokacin katsewa (ms) don karatun gudanarwa na cikin uwar garken MCP (lafiya, juriya, combos, quota, amfani).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_UPSTREAM_TIMEOUT_MS`             | `60000`                                             | `open-sse/mcp-server/fetchTimeout.ts`                       | Iyakar lokacin dakatarwa (ms) ga tsallaken MCP da ke jiran mai samarwa (`route_request`, `web_search`, `web_fetch`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_CORPUS_CACHE_SIZE`                   | `5`                                                 | `src/lib/localCorpus/configured.ts`                         | Matsakaicin adadin misalan fihirisar kundin bayanai na gida da ake adanawa a ƙwaƙwalwa (LRU, ɗaya ga kowace tushen kundin adireshi da aka yi wa fihirisa). Ana ɗaga shi zuwa mafi ƙarancin `1` idan ya yi ƙasa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `MODEL_SYNC_INTERVAL_HOURS`                     | `24`                                                | `src/shared/services/modelSyncScheduler.ts`                 | Tazarar daidaita kundin samfura cikin sa'o'i.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `PROVIDER_LIMITS_SYNC_INTERVAL_MINUTES`         | `70`                                                | `src/lib/usage/providerLimits.ts`                           | Tazarar duba iyakar ƙimar mai samarwa da ƙason amfani.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `PROVIDER_LIMITS_SYNC_SPACING_MS`               | `1500`                                              | `src/lib/usage/providerLimits.ts`                           | Tazara (ms) tsakanin ɗauko bayanan ƙason OAuth jere-jere a daidaitawar rukuni; ana ɗauko haɗin OAuth ɗaya bayan ɗaya don guje wa cunkushe uwar garken sama da buƙatu. `0` yana fita daga wannan tsarin (a lokaci guda).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_QUOTA_FETCH_MIN_INTERVAL_MS`         | `250`                                               | `open-sse/services/quotaFetchThrottle.ts`                   | Mafi ƙarancin tazara (ms) tsakanin ɗauko bayanan ƙaso jere-jere daga uwar garken sama a hanyar binciken farko/sa ido ta kowace buƙata; yana rarraba kiran hanyar sadarwa na lokaci guda don kada asusu da yawa a IP ɗaya su cunkushe uwar garken sama. An haɗa shi da masu ɗauko bayanan ƙaso na Codex (`/wham/usage`), DeepSeek, Bailian (duka wuraren ɗauko bayanai), OpenCode, da Crof (#6009, #6911). Har yanzu ba a rufe hanyar aika aikin gama-gari ta `usage.ts::getUsageForProvider` (github/glm/minimax/nanogpt/xai/etc.) ba — ana bibiyarta dabam. Ba ya shafar samun bayanai daga ma'ajiyar wucin gadi. `0` yana kashewa; ana iyakancewa zuwa `0..5000`. |

| `COMPRESSION_PIPELINE_BREAKER_COOLDOWN_MS`      | `30000`                                             | `open-sse/services/compression/pipelineEngineBreaker.ts`    | Adadin milisakannin da ake ci gaba da tsallake inji da breaker ɗinsa ya buɗe kafin gwajin rabin-buɗe.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `COMPRESSION_CCR_RETRIEVAL_RAMP_FACTOR`         | `2`                                                 | `open-sse/services/compression/engines/ccr/index.ts`        | Ƙarin martanin-dawo-da-bayanai na T08/H8 CCR: kowane dawo da wani tubalin da aka adana a baya yana ƙara ingantaccen `minChars` nasa a layi madaidaiciya (abubuwan da ake yawan dawo da su ba sa matsewa sosai; dawo da su sau `>=3` = ba za a taɓa matsawa ba). `1` yana kashe ƙarin (tsallakewa ta binary a bakin iyaka kawai).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `COMPRESSION_CCR_DURABLE_STORE`                 | `true`                                              | `open-sse/services/compression/engines/ccr/index.ts`        | Ma'ajiyar tubalan CCR mai ɗorewa (#9061). Tana tallafa wa ma'ajiyar cikin ƙwaƙwalwa da SQLite domin tubali ya ci gaba da kasancewa bayan korewar LRU, TTL, sake farawa, ko kuma buƙatar dawo da bayanai ta sauka a wata instance. Saita `false` domin ajiye tubalan a cikin ƙwaƙwalwa kawai. Tubalan da suka wuce 512KB da runtimes na cloud za su ci gaba da kasancewa a cikin ƙwaƙwalwa kawai ba tare da la'akari da wannan saitin ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `COMPRESSION_PREFIX_FREEZE_ENABLED`             | `false`                                             | `open-sse/services/compression/prefixFreeze.ts`             | Babban maɓallin daskare prefix bisa amfani da aka lura na T08/H5. **Sai an zaɓa (a kashe yake ta tsohuwa)** — idan yana kunne, system prompt da aka lura da shi sau `>=` iyakar za a ɗauke shi a matsayin tsayayyen prefix da za a iya cache, kuma a kiyaye shi daga compression har ma ga providers da static cache heuristic bai gano ba (daskarewa tana _kiyayewa_ kawai, ba ta taɓa canzawa).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `COMPRESSION_PREFIX_FREEZE_THRESHOLD`           | `3`                                                 | `open-sse/services/compression/prefixFreeze.ts`             | Yawan lura da system prompt kafin a ɗauke shi a matsayin tsayayyen prefix da aka daskare.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_BOOTSTRAPPED`                        | `false`                                             | `src/app/(dashboard)/dashboard/page.tsx`                    | Bootstrap script ne ke saita `true` bayan saitin farko. Yana sarrafa bayyanar setup wizard.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_BODY_PROJECT_OVERRIDE`         | `0`                                                 | `open-sse/executors/antigravity.ts`                         | Hanyar kaucewa: ba da damar request body ya maye gurbin project field na Antigravity.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_CREDITS`                           | `off`                                               | `open-sse/services/antigravityCredits.ts`                   | Manufofin Google One AI credits: `off` ba ya taɓa saka credits, `retry` yana saka su sau ɗaya bayan quota 429 da ya cancanta, sannan `always` yana saka su a request na farko.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_ALLOW_SIGNATURE_BYPASS`            | `0`                                                 | `open-sse/translator/request/openai-to-gemini.ts`           | Ba da damar Antigravity request translator ya tsallake tsauraran matakan tabbatar da request-signature na CLI idan upstream ya ƙi ingantattun signatures (yanayin debug/tsohon-CLI). Ƙima marar sifili tana kunna tsallakewar.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `AGY_TOKEN_FILE`                                | `~/.gemini/antigravity-cli/antigravity-oauth-token` | `src/app/api/providers/agy-auth/apply-local/route.ts`       | Maye gurbin hanyar token-file ta Antigravity CLI (agy) don shigo da local login da aka gano kai tsaye.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |

### Gadar OAuth CLI (Ta Ciki)

| Variable            | Tsoho             | Source File                     | Bayani                                          |
| ------------------- | ----------------- | ------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_SERVER`  | auto-detect       | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Server URL don gadar tantancewar CLI↔OmniRoute. |
| `OMNIROUTE_TOKEN`   | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Auth token don gadar CLI.                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_USER_ID` | `cli`             | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | User ID don zaman gadar CLI.                    |
| `SERVER_URL`        | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Tsohon alias na `OMNIROUTE_SERVER`.             |
| `CLI_TOKEN`         | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Tsohon alias na `OMNIROUTE_TOKEN`.              |
| `CLI_USER_ID`       | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Tsohon alias na `OMNIROUTE_USER_ID`.            |
