## 11. Takardun Shaida na Mai Ba da OAuth

Takardun shaida da aka gina don **ci gaban localhost**. Don tura nesa, yi rijistar naka a kowane na'urar wasan kwaikwayo na mai ba da sabis.

| Mai Canzawa                                 | Mai Ba da Sabis          | Bayanai                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| :------------------------------------------ | :----------------------- | :------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CLAUDE_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                    | Claude Code (Anthropic)  | Abokin ciniki na jama'a — babu buƙatar sirri.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_REDIRECT_URI`                  | Claude Code              | Sake tura URI. Tsoho: `https://platform.claude.com/oauth/code/callback`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CODEX_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                     | Codex / OpenAI           | Abokin ciniki na jama'a.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `GEMINI_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                    | Gemini (Google)          | Yana buƙatar daidaitawa `_SECRET`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `GEMINI_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`                | Gemini (Google)          | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `KIMI_CODING_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`               | Kimi Coding (Moonshot)   | Abokin ciniki na jama'a.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `MUSE_CODE_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                 | Muse Code (Meta)         | Zaɓi don maye gurbin ID na abokin ciniki na jama'a na Muse CLI. Bar shi ba a saita shi ba don amfani da abokin ciniki na jama'a da aka gina.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`               | Antigravity (Google)     | Yana buƙatar daidaitawa `_SECRET`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`           | Antigravity (Google)     | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `GITHUB_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                    | GitHub Copilot           | Abokin ciniki na jama'a.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `GHE_COPILOT_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`               | GHE Copilot              | Zaɓi don maye gurbin ID na abokin ciniki na OAuth na GitHub Enterprise Copilot. Yana komawa ga tsohon ID na `GITHUB_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID` lokacin da ba a saita shi ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `COPILOT_INTEGRATION_ID`                    | GitHub Copilot           | Zaɓi don maye gurbin ID na haɗin gwiwar abokin ciniki na GitHub Copilot da aka aiko a cikin `Copilot-Integration-Id` da `Editor-Plugin-Version` headers. Yana komawa ga `copilot-developer-cli`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `WINDSURF_API_KEY`                          | Windsurf / Devin (v3.8)  | Maɓallin API da `open-sse/executors/devin-cli.ts` ke amfani da shi lokacin da babu takardar shaidar kowane haɗi. Zaɓi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `CLI_DEVIN_BIN`                             | Devin CLI (v3.8)         | Hanyar al'ada zuwa binary na Devin CLI (`devin`). `open-sse/executors/devin-cli.ts` ne ke warware shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `GITLAB_DUO_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                | GitLab Duo (v3.8)        | ID na abokin ciniki na OAuth don GitLab Duo. Yi rijistar app a `https://gitlab.com/-/profile/applications` tare da sake tura URI `<NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL>/callback` da scopes `api, read_user, openid, profile, email`. Yana komawa ga `GITLAB_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `GITLAB_DUO_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`            | GitLab Duo (v3.8)        | Sirrin abokin ciniki na OAuth don GitLab Duo. Zaɓi — PKCE flow baya buƙatar sirri. Yana komawa ga `GITLAB_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `GITLAB_DUO_BASE_URL`                       | GitLab Duo (v3.8)        | Maye gurbin GitLab base URL (GitLab mai zaman kansa). Yana komawa ga `https://gitlab.com`. Yana komawa ga `GITLAB_BASE_URL`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `GITLAB_BASE_URL`                           | GitLab Duo (v3.8)        | Komawar gado don `GITLAB_DUO_BASE_URL`. Ana amfani da shi lokacin da aka cire bambancin `_DUO_`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `GITLAB_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                    | GitLab Duo (v3.8)        | Komawar gado don `GITLAB_DUO_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID` wanda `src/lib/oauth/constants/oauth.ts` ke amfani da shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `GITLAB_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`                | GitLab Duo (v3.8)        | Komawar gado don `GITLAB_DUO_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET` wanda `src/lib/oauth/constants/oauth.ts` ke amfani da shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `QODER_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`                 | Qoder                    | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `QODER_OAUTH_AUTHORIZE_URL`                 | Qoder                    | An saita don kunna Qoder OAuth.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `QODER_OAUTH_TOKEN_URL`                     | Qoder                    | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `QODER_OAUTH_USERINFO_URL`                  | Qoder                    | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `QODER_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                     | Qoder                    | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `QODER_PERSONAL_ACCESS_TOKEN`               | Qoder                    | Maɓallin API kai tsaye (yana kaucewa OAuth).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `QODER_CLI_WORKSPACE`                       | Qoder                    | ID na sararin aiki don Qoder CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_QODER_WORKSPACE`                 | Qoder                    | Sunan laƙabi don `QODER_CLI_WORKSPACE`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `QODER_CLI_CONFIG_DIR`                      | Qoder                    | Sauya kundin tsarin Qoder CLI (zaman PAT mai zaman kansa, yana kaucewa shiga mai bincike).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `BLACKBOX_WEB_VALIDATED_TOKEN`              | Blackbox Web             | Alamar `tk` ta gaba don aikawa a matsayin `validated` akan `/api/chat`. Ana buƙata lokacin da Blackbox ke tilasta daidaita alama; in ba haka ba OmniRoute yana komawa ga UUID bazuwar. Duba batu #2252.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `VISION_BRIDGE_BASE_URL`                    | Tsaron gadar hangen nesa | URL na tushe mai jituwa da OpenAI don kiran gadar hangen nesa marasa Anthropic. Yana komawa zuwa tsohon yanayin OpenAI URL ko api.openai.com. Nuna zuwa OmniRoute's `/v1` mai zaman kansa ko kowane ƙarshen OpenAI-compat (Gemini OpenAI-compat, OpenRouter). Batu #2232. Lokacin da URL ɗin shine OmniRoute's `/v1`, ƙaramin buƙatar bayanin yana aika `x-omniroute-admission-bypass: internal` kuma yana tantancewa tare da ingantaccen takardar shaidar mai zaman kansa (`sk_omniroute` mai tsaro a yanayin gida, ko `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY` / `ROUTER_API_KEY` — #1350) don haka `REQUIRE_API_KEY=true` ayyukan suna aiki. |
| `VISION_BRIDGE_API_KEY`                     | Tsaron gadar hangen nesa | Maɓallin API don URL ɗin da ke sama. Yana sauya kowane mai bayarwa OpenAI / Google env vars don kiran gadar hangen nesa marasa Anthropic. Samfuran Anthropic suna riƙe da hanyar maɓallin Anthropic ɗin su. Batu #2232.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_VISION_BRIDGE_NEGATIVE_CACHE_MS` | Tsaron gadar hangen nesa | Yaya tsawon lokacin da aka adana sakamakon hanyar "babu ɗan takara mai amfani", a cikin ms (tsoho `30000`). Ƙimar mara inganci ko mara kyau suna komawa ga tsoho; `0` yana kashe cache mara kyau. Tushe: `src/lib/guardrails/visionBridgeRouter.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |

> [!WARNING]
>
> 1. Je zuwa [Google Cloud Console → Credentials](https://console.cloud.google.com/apis/credentials)
> 2. Ƙirƙiri ID na Abokin ciniki na OAuth 2.0 (nau'i: "Aikace-aikacen yanar gizo")
> 3. Ƙara URL ɗin sabar ku a matsayin Authorized redirect URI
> 4. Sauya ƙimar takardar shaidar a cikin `.env`.

## 12. Sauya Ƙimar User-Agent na Mai Samarwa

Sauya kanun `User-Agent` da ake aikawa zuwa kowane mai samarwa na sama. Ajin tushe na executor ne ke tantance wannan a lokacin gudanarwa:

```
process.env[`${PROVIDER_ID}_USER_AGENT`]
```

> **Tushe:** `open-sse/executors/base.ts` → `buildHeaders()`

| Sauyi | Ƙimar Tsoho | Lokacin Sabuntawa |
| -------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `CLAUDE_USER_AGENT` | `claude-cli/2.1.258 (external, cli)` | Lokacin da Anthropic ya fitar da sabon sigar CLI |
| `CLAUDE_DISABLE_TOOL_NAME_CLOAK` | `false` | `executors/base.ts` + `executors/cliproxyapi.ts` | Saita zuwa `1`/`true` don tura sunayen kayan aikin harness na ɓangare na uku kai tsaye zuwa Anthropic a duka hanyoyin da ke zuwa Anthropic (OAuth na asali da CLIProxyAPI). Ta tsohuwa, executor yana sanya wa sunayen kayan aikin da ba na Claude Code ba laƙabi cikin ƙayyadadden tsari (yana amfani da daidaitaccen taswirar Claude Code idan akwai, in ba haka ba PascalCase), sannan yana mayar da sunayen zuwa na asali a cikin amsa ta hanyar `_toolNameMap`, don kada a ƙi harnesses masu kayan aikin snake_case a matsayin abokan hulɗa na ɓangare na uku da aka gano ta sawun yatsa. Don gyaran kurakurai kawai. |
| `CODEX_USER_AGENT` | `codex-cli/0.155.0 (Windows 10.0.26200; x64)` | Lokacin da OpenAI ya sabunta Codex CLI |
| `CODEX_CLIENT_VERSION` | `0.155.0` | Sauya sigar client ta Codex dabam da cikakken kirtanin UA |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_CLIENT_VERSION` | `2.1.258` | Sauya sigar Claude Code da ake sanarwa dabam da `CLAUDE_USER_AGENT`. Anthropic yana taƙaita wasu models bisa wannan ƙima (#12417). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_CLIENT_BUILD_REVISION` | `1e2` | Sauya kari mai haruffa 3 da OmniRoute ke liƙawa zuwa `cc_version=` a cikin ɓangaren lissafin kuɗin Claude. Ƙara shi tare da `CLAUDE_CODE_CLIENT_VERSION` — kulle sigar kaɗai yana sanar da haɗin `version.revision` wanda babu wani binary na ainihi da ke fitarwa. |
| `GITHUB_COPILOT_CLI_VERSION` | `1.0.81-6` | Sauya sigar Copilot CLI da ake sanarwa dabam da `GITHUB_USER_AGENT` |
| `GITHUB_USER_AGENT` | `GitHubCopilotChat/0.54.0` | Lokacin da GitHub Copilot Chat ya sabunta |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_USER_AGENT` | `antigravity/2.0.1 darwin/arm64` | Lokacin da Antigravity IDE ya sabunta |
| `KIRO_USER_AGENT` | `AWS-SDK-JS/3.0.0 kiro-ide/1.0.0` | Lokacin da Kiro IDE ya sabunta |
| `KIRO_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID` | `kiro-cli` | Sauya `clientId` (ID na jama'a) na lambar na'urar zamantakewa ta Kiro |
| `KIRO_VERIFY_FULL_CRC` | `false` | Zaɓin shiga: cikakken ingancin CRC na kowane frame a kan rafin event na Kiro (don gano matsalar rafukan da suka lalace) |
| `QODER_USER_AGENT` | `Qoder-Cli` | Lokacin da Qoder CLI ya sabunta |
| `CURSOR_USER_AGENT` | `Cursor/3.3` | Lokacin da Cursor ya sabunta |

> [!TIP]
> Za ka iya ƙara sauya ƙimar User-Agent ga **kowane** mai samarwa ta amfani da tsarin `{PROVIDER_ID}_USER_AGENT`. Executor yana gina sunan env var a lokacin gudanarwa.

## 13. Daidaituwar Sawun Yatsa na CLI

Idan aka kunna, OmniRoute yana sake tsara taken HTTP da filayen jikin JSON domin su dace da ainihin sa hannun kayan aikin CLI na hukuma. Wannan yana rage haɗarin yi wa asusu alama, tare da kiyaye IP na proxy ɗinka.

**Tushe:** `open-sse/config/cliFingerprints.ts`, `open-sse/executors/base.ts`

### Na Kowane Mai Bayarwa

| Sauyi                    | Kunnawa | Tasiri                                           |
| ------------------------ | ------- | ------------------------------------------------ |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CODEX`       | `=1`    | Yana kwaikwayon sa hannun buƙatar Codex CLI      |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CLAUDE`      | `=1`    | Yana kwaikwayon sa hannun buƙatar Claude Code    |
| `CLI_COMPAT_GITHUB`      | `=1`    | Yana kwaikwayon sa hannun buƙatar GitHub Copilot |
| `CLI_COMPAT_ANTIGRAVITY` | `=1`    | Yana kwaikwayon sa hannun buƙatar Antigravity    |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CURSOR`      | `=1`    | Yana kwaikwayon sa hannun buƙatar Cursor         |
| `CLI_COMPAT_KIMI_CODING` | `=1`    | Yana kwaikwayon sa hannun buƙatar Kimi Coding    |
| `CLI_COMPAT_KILOCODE`    | `=1`    | Yana kwaikwayon sa hannun buƙatar Kilo Code      |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CLINE`       | `=1`    | Yana kwaikwayon sa hannun buƙatar Cline          |

### Na Gabaɗaya

| Sauyi            | Kunnawa | Tasiri                                                             |
| ---------------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `CLI_COMPAT_ALL` | `=1`    | Kunna daidaituwar sawun yatsa ga **duk** masu bayarwa lokaci guda. |

### Sauya bayanan asalin Kimi Coding CLI

| Sauyi                   | Tsoho                   | Fayil ɗin Tushe                          | Bayani                                                                                |
| ----------------------- | ----------------------- | ---------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `KIMI_CLI_VERSION`      | `1.36.0`                | `src/lib/oauth/providers/kimi-coding.ts` | Sauya sigar Kimi CLI da ake aikawa yayin kiran OAuth/API.                             |
| `KIMI_CODING_DEVICE_ID` | _(tsohon da aka ɗauka)_ | `src/lib/oauth/providers/kimi-coding.ts` | Sauya ID na na'urar Kimi da aka ɗauka, wanda ake amfani da shi a taken abokin ciniki. |

> [!NOTE]
> Wannan fasalin yana aiki tare da sauye-sauyen User-Agent (§12). Tsarin sawun yatsa yana kula da jerin taken da jerin filayen jiki, yayin da sauye-sauyen User-Agent ke kula da takamaiman zaren UA. Ana iya kunna kowannensu da kansa.

---

## 14. Masu Bayar da Maɓallin API

Maɓallan API na masu bayarwa waɗanda ke amfani da tantancewa kai tsaye. **Saitin da aka fi so:** Dashboard → Providers → Add API Key.

Saita su ta hanyar sauye-sauyen muhalli wata hanya ce ta daban don girkawar Docker ko tsarin da ba shi da dubawar allo.

Tsarin da ake ganewa: `{PROVIDER_ID}_API_KEY`

| Sauyi              | Mai Bayarwa                                         |
| ------------------ | --------------------------------------------------- |
| `DEEPSEEK_API_KEY` | DeepSeek                                            |
| `NVIDIA_API_KEY`   | NVIDIA NIM                                          |
| `JINA_AI_API_KEY`  | Jina AI (Foundation API + madadin Reader)           |
| `JINA_API_KEY`     | Jina AI (laƙabi na `JINA_AI_API_KEY`)               |
| `GEMINI_API_KEY`   | Gemini (Google AI Studio) embeddings + madadin taɗi |
| `GOOGLE_API_KEY`   | Gemini (laƙabi na `GEMINI_API_KEY`)                 |

> [!NOTE]
> An cire shigarwar `${PROVIDER}_API_KEY` na dindindin don Groq, xAI, Mistral, Perplexity, Together AI, Fireworks, Cerebras, Cohere, Nebius, da Qianfan a v3.8.0 saboda runtime ba ya ƙara karanta su — waɗannan masu bayarwa suna dogara ne kawai da Dashboard / `data/provider-credentials.json` / DB mai ɓoyewa. Duba sashen _Bincike: Sauye-sauyen da Aka Cire / Marasa Amfani_ a ƙasan wannan takarda don hanyar ƙaura.

> [!TIP]
> Maɓallan da aka saita ta Dashboard ana adana su da ɓoyewa a SQLite kuma suna da fifiko kan sauye-sauyen muhalli.
>
> **Jina:** embeddings na `jina-ai/…`, rerank, classify, segment, da `jina-search` ba sa cajin maɓallin muhallin cluster idan akwai haɗin dashboard na `jina-ai` (ko `jina-reader` na bai ɗaya) — `getProviderCredentials` yana cike na farko. Ana amfani da `JINA_AI_API_KEY` / `JINA_API_KEY` ne kawai idan babu maɓallin dashboard mai amfani. Rajistan kira yana danganta madadin muhalli da `connection_id=env:JINA_AI_API_KEY`. Katin Reader (`jina-reader`, `r.jina.ai`) ba ya taɓa ba da `/v1/embeddings` ko `/v1/rerank`.
>
> **Gemini:** `gemini/gemini-embedding-2` (laƙabi `google/gemini-embedding-2`) yana fara amfani da haɗin `gemini` na dashboard. Ana amfani da `GEMINI_API_KEY` / `GOOGLE_API_KEY` ne kawai idan babu maɓallin dashboard mai amfani. Rajistan kira yana danganta madadin muhalli da `connection_id=env:GEMINI_API_KEY`. Zirga-zirgar multimodal ta asali tana amfani da `x-goog-api-key` zuwa `:embedContent` / `:batchEmbedContents` — abubuwan OpenAI `input` guda N suna zama vectors guda N.

---

## 15. Saitunan Ƙarewar Lokaci

Dukkan ƙimomi suna cikin **milisekan**. Ana warware su a wuri guda cikin `src/shared/utils/runtimeTimeouts.ts`.

### Tsarin Matsayin Ƙarewar Lokaci

```
REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS (sauyin da ya shafi duka)
├─→ FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS (kiran masu samar da sabis na waje, tsoho: 600000)
│   ├─→ FETCH_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS (yana gado daga FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS)
│   ├─→ FETCH_BODY_TIMEOUT_MS (yana gado daga FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS)
│   ├─→ TLS_CLIENT_TIMEOUT_MS (yana gado daga FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS)
│   │   └── TLS_FIRST_BYTE_WATCHDOG_MS (mai zaman kansa, tsoho: 10000)
│   ├── RESPONSES_FIRST_BYTE_TIMEOUT_MS (mai zaman kansa, tsoho: 15000)
│   ├── OPENCODE_RESPONSES_HEADERS_WAIT_MS (mai zaman kansa, tsoho: 0 = a kashe, shawara: 30000)
│   ├── OPENCODE_RESPONSES_HEADERS_WAIT_MAX_ROTATIONS (mai zaman kansa, tsoho: 2)
│   ├── FETCH_CONNECT_TIMEOUT_MS (mai zaman kansa, tsoho: 30000)
│   └── FETCH_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS (mai zaman kansa, tsoho: 4000)
├─→ STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS (yana gado daga REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS, tsoho: 600000)
├─→ STREAM_ACTIVE_TIMEOUT_MS (mai zaman kansa, tsoho: 1260000; 0 yana kashe shi)
├─→ STREAM_READINESS_TIMEOUT_MS (yana gado daga REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS, tsoho: 80000)
├─→ STREAM_READINESS_MAX_TIMEOUT_MS (yana iyakance tsawaitawar shiri mai daidaitawa, tsoho: 180000)
└─→ API_BRIDGE_PROXY_TIMEOUT_MS (yana gado daga REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS, tsoho: 30000)
    ├─→ API_BRIDGE_SERVER_REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS (an samo shi, tsoho: 300000)
    ├── API_BRIDGE_SERVER_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS (tsoho: 60000)
    ├── API_BRIDGE_SERVER_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS (tsoho: 5000)
    └── API_BRIDGE_SERVER_SOCKET_TIMEOUT_MS (tsoho: 0 = an kashe)
```

| Sauyi                                           | Tsoho                                                             | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| ----------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS`                            | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                 | Gajeriyar hanya ta gaba ɗaya — tana maye gurbin tsoffin ƙimomin `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS` da `STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                              | `600000`                                                          | Jimillar wa'adin lokacin buƙatar HTTP don kiraye-kirayen mai bayarwa na sama.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS`                        | `600000`                                                          | Matsakaicin lokacin shiru tsakanin ɗanyen bytes na sama kafin a katse. Samfuran tsawaitaccen-tunani ba safai suke dakatawa >90s ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `STREAM_ACTIVE_TIMEOUT_MS`                      | `1260000`                                                         | Matsakaicin jimillar tsawon rayuwar rafin SSE mai aiki; ba ya sake farawa saboda bytes na sama kuma bai dogara da `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS` ba. An samo shi daga mafi girman `timeoutMs` na kowane samfuri a rajista (1200000, Codex) tare da ƙarin tazara ta 60000, don kada a taɓa kashe samfurin da aka ba shi damar amfani da cikakken kasafinsa a tsakiyar amsa. Saita zuwa `0` don kashe shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_SSE_COMMENTS`                        | _(a kashe)_                                                       | Ko OmniRoute na iya fitar da layukan sharhin SSE `:` (misali, bugun-zuciyar `: keepalive` da ƙarshen metadata na `x-omniroute-*`). An kashe shi ta tsohuwa (#10524) saboda tsauraran abokan ciniki masu dacewa da OpenAI suna yin JSON.parse ga kowane layin SSE kuma suna rushewa kan sharhin `:`; wannan ba ya shafar bugun-zuciya na `data:`. Saita `on`/`true`/`1`/`yes` don sake ba da izini. `open-sse/utils/sseHeartbeat.ts` ne ke amfani da shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `STREAM_READINESS_TIMEOUT_MS`                   | `80000`                                                           | Lokacin karɓar aukuwar SSE ta farko wadda ba ping ba. Yana gādon `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS` idan an saita shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `STREAM_READINESS_MAX_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `180000`                                                          | Matsakaicin taga mai daidaitawa na jiran aukuwar farko don manyan buƙatun rafawa, masu yawan amfani da kayan aiki, ko masu zurfin tunani.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_AGENT_GOAL_POLICY_ENABLED`           | `true`                                                            | Makunnin kashewa na tsarin hasashen `/goal`. Saita `false`/`0`/`off` don kashe ganowa gaba ɗaya — ba za a taɓa ƙara wa'adin shirin aiki da farfaɗo da rafi ta jikin buƙata/kanun labarai ba, don rage yiwuwar abokin ciniki ya ƙara wa'adin lokaci yadda yake so.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_AGENT_GOAL_READINESS_MAX_TIMEOUT_MS` | `600000`                                                          | Matsakaicin taga na jiran aukuwar farko don ayyukan wakilin `/goal` da aka gano ko buƙatun da aka tilasta da `x-omniroute-agent-goal`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_AGENT_GOAL_STREAM_RECOVERY`          | `true`                                                            | Kunna farfaɗo da rafi da wuri ta atomatik don ayyukan wakilin `/goal` da aka gano. Saita `false`/`0`/`off` don kashe izinin shiga na musamman ga goal. Wannan zai iya ƘARA farfaɗowa ne kawai a kan tsohon saitin mai gudanarwa — ba ya taɓa soke ficewa da aka bayyana sarai ta saitunan `STREAM_RECOVERY_ENABLED`/DB.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_DROP_NONSTANDARD_EVENTS`       | `true`                                                            | Cire abubuwan SSE marasa daidaito na `codex.*` (misali, `codex.rate_limits`) waɗanda ke karya `responses.stream()` na OpenAI SDK tare da 502. A KUNNE ta tsohuwa (#11014). Saita `0`/`false`/`no`/`off` don tura su gaba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS`                  | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                 | Hanyar sufuri ta Codex app-server mai buƙatar zaɓin shiga. Mahadar WebSocket (`ws://`/`wss://`) ta sidecar na `codex app-server` na gida. Idan aka saita shi tare da token, ana karkatar da buƙatun Codex ta JSON-RPC zuwa sidecar maimakon HTTP Responses API. Haka kuma ana iya saita shi ga kowace haɗi ta `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerUrl`. `open-sse/executors/codex/appServerConfig.ts` ne ke amfani da shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS_TOKEN`            | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                 | Token na izini/bearer na cikin layi da ake gabatarwa ga app-server. Maye gurbin kowane haɗi: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerToken`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS_TOKEN_FILE`       | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                 | Hanyar fayil mai ɗauke da token na izinin app-server (daga `codex app-server --ws-token-file`). Ana amfani da shi idan ba a saita `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS_TOKEN` ba. Maye gurbin kowane haɗi: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerTokenFile`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_CWD`                 | `/tmp`                                                            | Kundin aiki da zagayen app-server ke gudana a ciki. Maye gurbin kowane haɗi: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerCwd`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_APPROVAL`            | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                 | Manufar amincewa da ake miƙawa zagayen app-server (misali, `never`, `on-request`). Maye gurbin kowane haɗi: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerApprovalPolicy`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_SANDBOX`             | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                 | Manufar sandbox da ake miƙawa zagayen app-server (misali, `read-only`, `workspace-write`, `danger-full-access`). Idan ba a saita ba, executor yana amfani da `workspace-write` a matsayin tsoho (an ƙarfafa tsaro; a baya `danger-full-access`). Maye gurbin kowane haɗi: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerSandbox`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_AUTO_APPROVE`        | `false`                                                           | Amincewa ta atomatik da buƙatun amincewa na app-server kansa (aiwatar da umarni/fayil/izini a kan na'urar mai masaukin baki). A kashe yake ta tsohuwa — ana ƙin buƙatun ta atomatik; wannan ba ya shafar kiraye-kirayen kayan aikin harness (suna bi ta dabam ta hanyar wucewar `item/tool/call`). Yana karɓar `true`/`1`/`yes`. Maye gurbin kowane haɗi: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerAutoApprove`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `FETCH_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`                      | = `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                                              | Lokacin karɓar kanun labaran amsa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_FETCH_START_TIMEOUT_CAP_MS`          | `110000`                                                          | Matsakaicin lokacin jiran kanun amsa a buƙatun yawo (ms). `0` yana kashe wannan iyaka; ƙayyadadden `fetchStartTimeoutCapMs` na mai samarwa yana da fifiko.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`           | `30000` (30s)                                                     | Matsakaicin lokacin jiran fara amsa (ms) ga kowane yunƙurin kai-tsaye ba tare da proxy ba. Idan lokaci ya ƙare, ana sake gwadawa sau ɗaya a kan sabon socket; saita `0` don kashe iyakar da kuma riƙe halayyar da ta gabata.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOCAL_DIRECT_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`     | `300000` (5 min)                                                  | Mafi ƙarancin lokacin jiran fara amsa (ms) ga yunƙurin kai-tsaye ba tare da proxy ba waɗanda uwar garken da ake nufi take ta sirri/LAN/loopback (misali `localhost`, `host.docker.internal`, `192.168.x.x`), domin kada tsohon farawar Ollama na asali ya katse saboda tsohon saitin 30s da ke sama. Yana aiki a matsayin mafi ƙarancin iyaka: ainihin lokacin jira shi ne mafi girma tsakanin wannan ƙimar da `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`; `0` ko ƙima mara inganci tana cire mafi ƙarancin iyakar gida. Wannan ba ya shafar uwar garken nesa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_RESPONSE_RETRY_TIMEOUT_MS`    | `600000` (10 min)                                                 | Mafi girman iyaka (ms) ga yunƙurin SAKE GWADAWA ta sabon socket bayan ƙarewar lokacin fara amsa na yunƙurin haɗin da aka tara a sama (#13703). Yana aiki ne kawai idan mai kira ya riga ya haɗa nasa siginar wa'adi (jerin fifikon haɗin da aka warware/model/mai samarwa/`FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`); wannan siginar ita ce ainihin iyaka kuma ita ce ke fara aiki a hanyar da aka nufa, don haka wannan kariya ce mai yalwa maimakon tsayayyen iyaka — ba tare da ita ba, sake gwadawar tana sake amfani da gajeriyar taga iri ɗaya ta `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS` kamar yunƙurin da aka tara, tana kuma mayar da 504 ga lafiyayyun model na tunani masu jinkirin TTFB. Ba a taɓa yarda ta kasance ƙasa da tsayayyen mafi ƙarancin iyakar da ke sama ba; idan mai kira bai samar da wata siginar wa'adi kwata-kwata ba, sake gwadawar tana barin tsayayyen mafi ƙarancin iyakar ba tare da canji ba. |
| `FETCH_BODY_TIMEOUT_MS`                         | = `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                                              | Lokacin karɓar cikakken jikin amsa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `FETCH_CONNECT_TIMEOUT_MS`                      | `30000`                                                           | Lokacin ƙarewar kafa haɗin TCP.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `FETCH_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS`                    | `4000`                                                            | Lokacin ƙarewar zaman jiran socket na keep-alive.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `TLS_CLIENT_TIMEOUT_MS`                         | = `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                                              | Lokacin ƙarewar proxy na sawun yatsan TLS (wreq-js).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `TLS_FIRST_BYTE_WATCHDOG_MS`                    | `10000`                                                           | Yana iyakance lokacin samun byte na farko musamman daga jikin hanyar jigilar sawun yatsan TLS ta wreq-js; `TLS_CLIENT_TIMEOUT_MS` kaɗai ba zai iya gano jikin da ya tsaya cak ba domin yana kammala da zarar kanun sun iso (#12656). Ƙarewar lokaci tana soke mai karanta wreq sannan ta koma ga mai rarraba kai-tsaye/proxy; `0` yana kashe mai sa ido.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `RESPONSES_FIRST_BYTE_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `15000`                                                           | Na mai aiwatar da OpenCode kawai, kuma kawai yayin da tutar fasalin `OPENCODE_RESPONSES_STALL_ROTATION` take kunne (a tsoho a kashe): yana iyakance jiran byte na farko na jikin amsar Responses mai yawo bayan kanunta (#13484). Yawon Responses yana farawa da `response.created`, don haka yin shiru fiye da wannan taga yana nufin tsayawa cak: ana sanyaya asusun kuma buƙatar tana zagayawa zuwa asusu na gaba sau ɗaya; tsayawa cak karo na biyu yana gaza nan take. `0` yana kashe kariyar ko da tutar tana kunne.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OPENCODE_RESPONSES_HEADERS_WAIT_MS`            | `0` (= a kashe)                                                   | Na mai aiwatar da OpenCode kawai: yana iyakance jiran kanun amsa daga tushe a kiran Responses mai yawo idan har yanzu akwai wani asusu — buƙatar da ke cikin layi tana daina jira ta ci gaba maimakon riƙe cikakkiyar tagar kanun. Kiran Responses masu yawo kawai abin ya shafa (yawon Responses yana farawa da `response.created`, don haka yin shiru a nan yana nufin layin jira ne, ba samarwa ba); ba a taɓa kammalawar hira da kira marasa yawo ba, kuma tagar tana rage ainihin iyakar fara fetch kawai, ba ta taɓa tsawaita shi ba. Ƙimar da aka ba da shawara idan ana kunnawa: `30000`. `0` yana kashewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OPENCODE_RESPONSES_HEADERS_WAIT_MAX_ROTATIONS` | `2`                                                               | Na mai aiwatar da OpenCode kawai: adadin lokutan da iyakar jiran kanun da ke sama za ta iya matsawa zuwa asusu na gaba a kowace buƙata. Asusun ƙarshe da ya rage kullum yana riƙe cikakkiyar tagar kanun.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OPENCODE_PARK_AND_RESUME`                      | `false`                                                           | Na mai aiwatar da OpenCode kawai: ajiye buƙatar tare da bugun zuciya bayan maimaitattun 429 na ɗan lokaci (ko sabon alamar matsin pool), sannan sake kunna ƙayyadadden zango guda ɗaya na har zuwa asusu 3 a jere maimakon baza buƙatar ga dukkan rukunin (#13924). A kashe yake a tsoho: kowane 429 yana zagayawa zuwa asusu na gaba daidai yadda yake a baya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `STREAM_READINESS_STALL_RETRY`                  | `false`                                                           | Hirar yawo kawai: idan jikin farko daga tushe ya tsaya cak kafin samar da event mai amfani, yi yunƙuri na biyu mai iyaka ta hanyar routing iri ɗaya, da kasafin shiri iri ɗaya kuma ba tare da hukunta asusu ba. A kashe yake a tsoho: jikin farko da ya tsaya cak yana sa buƙatar ta gaza ba tare da sake gwadawa ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OPENCODE_POOL_RESELECT`                        | `false`                                                           | Na mai aiwatar da OpenCode kawai: bayan 429 daga mai samarwa da aka ware bisa egress ga asusun da ba shi da proxy a ƙarƙashin mahallin pool na kewaye, nemi connection pool ya samar da wani memba don yunƙuri na gaba maimakon sake gwada adireshin egress ɗin nan. Yana tsara fifiko, ba ya cirewa: pool da ya ƙare yana ci gaba da halin yanzu. A kashe yake a tsoho: kowane 429 yana zagayawa zuwa asusu na gaba daidai yadda yake a baya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OPENCODE_POOL_STRAIN_MARKER_PATH`              | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                 | Na mai aiwatar da OpenCode kawai: sauya path na alamar matsin pool da ake karantawa kafin ajiye buƙata (`{since, reason, ttl_s}`, tsoho `/tmp/opencode-pool-strain.json`, #13924). Sabuwar alama tana ajiye buƙatar ba tare da sake ƙirga ba; idan babu ko ta tsufa, sai a koma ga mai ƙirga yawaitar buƙatu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `API_BRIDGE_PROXY_TIMEOUT_MS`                   | `30000`                                                           | Iyakar lokacin tsallen proxy don buƙatun gada na `/v1`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `FIRECRAWL_BASE_URL`                            | `https://api.firecrawl.dev`                                       | Nuna mai aiwatar da ɗebo bayanan yanar gizo na Firecrawl zuwa wani instance da aka masaukinsa da kai (maɓallin API ba dole ba ne idan ba a cloud ba).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |

| `API_BRIDGE_SERVER_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS`        | `5000`                                                            | Iyakar lokacin zaman banza na keep-alive na gadar.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `API_BRIDGE_SERVER_SOCKET_TIMEOUT_MS`           | `0`                                                               | Iyakar lokacin raw socket (0 = an kashe).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |

| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_PROBE_TIMEOUT_MS`           | `8000`                                                            | Iyakar lokaci (ms) don saitunan `validationRead` da `modelsProbe` a cikin `src/shared/network/safeOutboundFetch.ts`.
