| `OMNIROUTE_RELAY_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`              | `25000`                                                           | Iyakar lokacin fetch ta musamman ga relay a cikin `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts` (#9158). Dole ne relay da ya maƙale ya gaza kafin iyakar lokacin client/agent (~30s), domin masu kira su ga gazawa ta musamman ga relay maimakon iyakar lokacin upstream na gama-gari. An iyakance shi zuwa `29000` domin koyaushe ya fara aiki.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_RETRY_BACKOFF_MS`                    | `10`                                                              | Jinkirin sake gwadawa na bai ɗaya don hanyoyin sake-gwadawa-sau-ɗaya na direct/relay/proxy a cikin `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts` (#9158). `0` = sake gwadawa nan take.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLAUDE_TLS_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `60000`                                                           | Iyakar lokacin buƙatar native wreq-js (`claudeTlsClient.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLAUDE_TLS_GRACE_MS`                 | `10000`                                                           | Ƙarin lokacin rangwamen cikakken wa’adin ƙarshe na JS da ake ƙarawa a kan iyakar lokacin native.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_PPLX_TLS_TIMEOUT_MS`                 | `30000`                                                           | Iyakar lokacin buƙatar native wreq-js (`perplexityTlsClient.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_PPLX_TLS_GRACE_MS`                   | `10000`                                                           | Ƙarin lokacin rangwamen cikakken wa’adin ƙarshe na JS da ake ƙarawa a kan iyakar lokacin native.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_PPLX_SEARCH_HINT`                    | `0` (a kashe)                                                     | Yana liƙa "Kana da ginannen binciken yanar gizo. Amsa tambayoyi kai tsaye ta amfani da sakamakon bincike." zuwa saƙon system na mai kira (`perplexity-web/protocol.ts`). A kashe yake ta tsohuwa — Perplexity yana yin bincike ko ta yaya, kuma jimlar tana bayyana a cikin amsoshi a matsayin tsokacin meta ga coding clients. Saita `1`/`true`/`yes`/`on` don maidowa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_GROK_TLS_TIMEOUT_MS`                 | `60000`                                                           | Iyakar lokacin buƙatar native wreq-js (`grokTlsClient.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_GROK_TLS_GRACE_MS`                   | `10000`                                                           | Ƙarin lokacin rangwamen cikakken wa’adin ƙarshe na JS da ake ƙarawa a kan iyakar lokacin native.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_NOTION_TLS_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `30000`                                                           | Iyakar lokacin buƙatar native wreq-js (`notionTlsClient.ts`); `notion-web` yana ɗaga shi ga kowace buƙata zuwa `180000` don dogayen samarwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_NOTION_TLS_GRACE_MS`                 | `10000`                                                           | Ƙarin lokacin rangwamen cikakken wa’adin ƙarshe na JS da ake ƙarawa a kan iyakar lokacin native.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_BROWSER_POOL`                        | `on`                                                              | Shared Playwright browser pool don hirar web-cookie mai amfani da browser (`browserPool.ts`); saita `off` don kashewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OBSCURA_BIN`                                   | `auto-detect`                                                     | Hanyar zuwa binary na `obscura` da browser pool da mai aiwatar da Cloudflare Playground ke amfani da shi a matsayin injin farko (`open-sse/services/obscura.ts`); ana gano shi ta atomatik daga PATH na tsarin idan ba a saita ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OBSCURA_CDP_ENDPOINT`                          | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                 | Nuna zuwa Obscura da ya riga yake aiki (`http://host:port`) maimakon ƙaddamar da wani; wannan module ba ya mallakar wannan process ɗin (`open-sse/services/obscura.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OBSCURA_PORT`                                  | `random free port`                                                | Takamaiman port don `obscura serve` da aka ƙaddamar; ana zaɓar port marar amfani ta atomatik idan ba a saita shi ba (`open-sse/services/obscura.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `WEB_COOKIE_USE_BROWSER`                        | `0`                                                               | Shigar da buƙatar web-cookie chat cikin hanyar da browser ke tallafawa (`browserBackedChat.ts`); yi amfani da `1` don kunnawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `KIMI_WEB_BASE_URL`                             | `https://www.kimi.ai`                                             | Asalin URL na mai aiwatar da Kimi Web (Connect-RPC na kimi.ai na ƙasa da ƙasa) (`kimi-web.ts`); a sauya shi kawai don endpoints na mirror/proxy.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `KIMI_WEB_CHAT_URL`                             | `<KIMI_WEB_BASE_URL>/apiv2/kimi.gateway.chat.v1.ChatService/Chat` | Cikakken chat endpoint na mai aiwatar da Kimi Web (`kimi-web.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOGIN_BROWSER_PATH`                  | _(ana gano shi ta atomatik)_                                      | Path zuwa executable na Chrome/Edge na tsarin don shiga Adobe Firefly ta browser mai mu'amala (`adobeFireflyBrowserLogin.ts`); yana maye gurbin ganowa ta atomatik na kowane OS.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_STANDALONE_DIR`                      | _.build/ standalone output_                                       | Sauyawar lokacin build don standalone output directory da matakin colocation bayan build ke amfani da shi (`scripts/build/colocate-standalone.mjs`); kayan aikin build ne, ba na runtime ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |

Ƙoƙarin combo target suna gado resolved upstream request timeout (`FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`, ko
`REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS` lokacin da yake samar da fetch default). Saita `targetTimeoutMs` a cikin combo,
combo defaults, ko provider override kawai don sa combo fallback ya yi sauri; ƙimomin da suka haura
upstream timeout na yanzu ana iyakance su zuwa upstream timeout ɗin.

`comboTimeoutMs` wani keɓantaccen whole-combo wall-clock budget ne a duk failover targets.
Bar shi ba a saita ba ko `0` don ci gaba da iteration mara iyaka (hardcoded hang-stop na mintuna 10 a
`comboPredicates.ts` har yanzu yana aiki). Ƙima mai kyau tana maye gurbin wannan
safety net ɗin ga combo. Ka tabbatar `comboTimeoutMs` ya fi `targetTimeoutMs` tsawo domin failover
ya ci gaba da samun lokaci bayan target na farko mai jinkiri.

### Iyakokin Circuit Breaker

Daidaita circuit breaker a matakin provider. Defaults suna nuna scaled values da ake amfani da su tun daga v3.6 don connections 500+.

| Sauyi                                                         | Tsoho     | Fayil ɗin Tushe                | Bayani                                                                                                                                                       |
| ------------------------------------------------------------- | --------- | ------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |

## 16. Rajista

Tsarin rajista yana rubutawa zuwa stdout da kuma fayilolin rajista masu juyawa. Ana karanta dukkan saituna daga `src/lib/logEnv.ts`.

| Sauyi                                     | Tsoho                      | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| ----------------------------------------- | -------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `APP_LOG_LEVEL`                           | `info`                     | Mafi ƙarancin matakin rajista: `debug`, `info`, `warn`, `error`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `APP_LOG_FORMAT`                          | `text`                     | Tsarin fitarwa: `text` (mai sauƙin karantawa ga mutum) ko `json` (mai tsari).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `APP_LOG_TO_FILE`                         | `true`                     | Rubuta rajista zuwa fayil tare da stdout.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `APP_LOG_FILE_PATH`                       | `logs/application/app.log` | Hanyar fayil ɗin rajista (dangane da tushen aikin ko `DATA_DIR`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `APP_LOG_MAX_FILE_SIZE`                   | `50M`                      | Matsakaicin girman fayil kafin juyawa. Yana karɓar: `50M`, `1G`, `512K`, ko bayit kai tsaye.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `APP_LOG_RETENTION_DAYS`                  | `7`                        | Yawan kwanakin adana fayilolin rajistar manhaja da aka juya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `APP_LOG_MAX_FILES`                       | `20`                       | Matsakaicin adadin kwafin ajiyar fayilolin rajista da aka juya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `CALL_LOG_RETENTION_DAYS`                 | `7`                        | Yawan kwanakin adana shigarwar rajistar buƙata/kira a cikin rumbun bayanai.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `CALL_LOG_MAX_ENTRIES`                    | `10000`                    | Matsakaicin adadin shigarwar rajistar kira a cikin ma'ajiyar ƙwaƙwalwar wucin gadi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CALL_LOGS_TABLE_MAX_ROWS`                | `100000`                   | Matsakaicin adadin layuka a teburin SQLite na `call_logs` kafin rage su.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `ENABLE_REQUEST_LOGS`                     | _(ba a saita ba)_          | Tilasta kunna ko kashe cikakken rajistar buƙata, tare da ƙetare saitin dashboard.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `MAX_PENDING_REQUEST_AGE_MS`              | `3600000` (awa 1)          | Matsakaicin shekarun shigarwar rajistar buƙata mai aiki da ta zama marar mai ita kafin tsaftace ƙwaƙwalwar wucin gadi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `CALL_LOG_PIPELINE_CAPTURE_STREAM_CHUNKS` | `false`                    | Adana gutsattsarin kwarara a cikin kayayyakin pipeline lokacin da `call_log_pipeline_enabled=true`. Dole ne a zaɓi kunnawa (`true`) — a kashe yake ta tsohuwa don adana sararin faifai.                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CALL_LOG_PIPELINE_MAX_SIZE_KB`           | `512`                      | Matsakaicin girman kayan rajistar kiran pipeline a KB lokacin da `call_log_pipeline_enabled=true`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `PROXY_LOGS_TABLE_MAX_ROWS`               | `100000`                   | Matsakaicin adadin layuka a teburin SQLite na `proxy_logs` kafin rage su.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `PROXY_LOG_INCLUDE_IPS`                   | `false`                    | Haɗa IP na abokin ciniki/fita da farkon asusu a cikin rajistocin console na `[ProxyEgress]`. Bayanan rajistar proxy na dashboard/rumbun bayanai suna riƙe da cikakkun bayanai.                                                                                                                                                             |
| `PROXY_LOG_FIRST_CHUNK_TIMING`            | `false`                    | Saita zuwa `"true"` ko `"1"` don yin rikodin `first_chunk_ms` (daga fara aikawa zuwa bayit na farko mai amfani na jiki) a layukan rajistar proxy. Yana naɗe kowane jikin upstream a cikin lazy passthrough stream sannan ya gyara layin da zarar bayit ɗin ya iso; a kashe yake ta tsohuwa domin martani su wuce ba tare da an taɓa su ba. |
| `APP_LOG_ROTATION_CHECK_INTERVAL_MS`      | `60000` (minti 1)          | Sau nawa `src/lib/logRotation.ts` ke sake duba girman fayil ɗin rajista mai aiki.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `CHAT_LOG_TEXT_LIMIT`                     | `65536`                    | Matsakaicin tsawon kirtani da ake riƙewa a cikin kayayyakin rajistar hira (tsoho 64 KB).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CHAT_LOG_ARRAY_TAIL_ITEMS`               | `128`                      | Adadin abubuwan jeri da ake riƙewa daga ƙarshen jeri lokacin gajarta payload na rajistar hira.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `CHAT_LOG_MAX_DEPTH`                      | `6`                        | Matsakaicin zurfin kafa-cikin-kafa kafin a gajarta payload na rajistar hira.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `CHAT_LOG_MAX_OBJECT_KEYS`                | `80`                       | Matsakaicin adadin maɓallan object da ake riƙewa a cikin payload na rajistar hira (0 = babu iyaka).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CHAT_LOG_MAX_BODY_KB`                    | `1024`                     | Girman dukkan jikin buƙata/martani (KB) kafin a maye gurbinsa da taƙaitaccen bayani kawai maimakon cikakken kwafi. Ƙara wannan idan dogayen tattaunawar wakili suna nuna alamar riƙe wuri maimakon ainihin saƙonni a dashboard.                                                                                                            |
| `CHAT_DEBUG_FILE`                         | `false`                    | Lokacin da yake true, `serializeArtifactForStorage` yana tsallake gajartawa bisa girma. Don gyaran kurakurai kawai.                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |

## 17. Inganta Amfani da Ƙwaƙwalwa

| Canji                         | Tsoho                                                                 | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| ----------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB`         | _na atomatik_ (kan na’ura kai tsaye); **`1024` a cikin hoton Docker** | **Ƙimar da aka ba da shawara** ta iyakar heap na V8 (MB) don Docker/standalone. Idan ba a saita shi ba, ana daidaita shi ta atomatik (~35% na RAM na tsarin, tare da taƙaita shi zuwa `[512, 4096]`); `512` shi ne kawai mafi ƙarancin ƙima idan ba za a iya karanta jimillar ƙwaƙwalwar ba. A kan `run-standalone.mjs` (Docker CMD), ana ƙara ƙimar da aka **bayyana kai tsaye** a matsayin `--max-old-space-size`, kuma ita ce ke **rinjayar** tutar heap ta NODE_OPTIONS da ke saɓani da ita (tutar V8 ta ƙarshe). Har yanzu `omniroute serve` yana fifita tutar heap ta NODE_OPTIONS da ta riga ta kasance (#5238). Kada a saita su biyun da lambobi daban-daban — tsarin yana rubuta gargaɗi da ke ambaton ƙimomin biyu da wadda ta yi rinjaye. **Hoton Docker na hukuma koyaushe yana saita `1024`, don haka ba a taɓa gudanar da daidaitawar a cikinsa.** Coding-agent `/v1/responses` yana buƙatar `8192`–`12288` tare da ƙarin sarari na cgroup — duba [Jagorar Docker — RAM na lokacin aiki](../guides/DOCKER_GUIDE.md#runtime-ram-for-coding-agents). |
| `PROMPT_CACHE_MAX_SIZE`       | `50`                                                                  | Matsakaicin adadin shigarwar system prompt da aka adana a cache.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `PROMPT_CACHE_MAX_BYTES`      | `2097152` (2 MB)                                                      | Matsakaicin jimillar girman cache na prompt.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `PROMPT_CACHE_TTL_MS`         | `300000` (minti 5)                                                    | TTL na shigarwar cache na prompt.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_SIZE`     | `100`                                                                 | Matsakaicin adadin amsoshin temperature=0 da aka adana a cache.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_BYTES`    | `4194304` (4 MB)                                                      | Matsakaicin jimillar girman semantic cache.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `SEMANTIC_CACHE_TTL_MS`       | `1800000` (minti 30)                                                  | TTL na shigarwar semantic cache.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_CORPUS_CACHE_SIZE` | `5`                                                                   | Tushen local-corpus da ke riƙe da index mai aiki a ƙwaƙwalwa a lokaci guda (`src/lib/localCorpus/configured.ts`). LRU: idan an kai iyakar, ana cire index na tushen da aka fi daɗewa ba a yi amfani da shi ba, sannan a sake gina shi a tambayarsa ta gaba. Ana taƙaita shi zuwa mafi ƙarancin `1`; ƙimar da ba lamba ba tana komawa zuwa tsohuwar ƙima.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `STREAM_HISTORY_MAX`          | `50`                                                                  | Matsakaicin adadin abubuwan stream na baya-bayan nan a cikin buffer na kallon kai tsaye na Dashboard.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `CONTEXT_LENGTH_DEFAULT`      | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                     | Sauya tsawon mahalli na gaba ɗaya (tokens) ga kowane provider da ba shi da canjin `CONTEXT_LENGTH_<PROVIDER>`. Idan an saita shi, yana **maye gurbin** ƙimomin catalog, sauyin Dashboard da registry ga waɗannan providers; idan ba a saita shi ba, OmniRoute yana amfani da jerin matakansa na ciki, wanda mafita ta ƙarshe ita ce `128000`. Duba [Tsawon mahalli na kowane provider](#per-provider-context-length-context_length_provider).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `USAGE_TOKEN_BUFFER`          | `100`                                                                 | Ƙarin sararin tokens da aka tanada yayin bin diddigin iyakokin amfani.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |

### Tsawon mahalli na kowane provider (`CONTEXT_LENGTH_<PROVIDER>`)

`open-sse/services/contextManager.ts` (`getEnvOverride()` / `resolveTokenLimit()`) kuma yana karanta
nau’in **kowane provider** wanda ake gina sunansa daga ID na provider a lokacin aiki, don haka ba za a iya
jera shi layi-layi a teburin da ke sama ko a cikin `.env.example` ba.

**Suna.** `CONTEXT_LENGTH_<PROVIDER>`, inda `<PROVIDER>` yake ID na provider da aka mayar da shi zuwa manyan
haruffa, tare da maye gurbin kowane harafin da ba ya cikin `A-Z` / `0-9` da `_`. ID na provider shi ne na canonical bayan
warware alias (ɓangaren `provider` na rubutun `provider/model` bayan an warware gajeren alias).
Nodes na musamman masu dacewa da OpenAI ko Anthropic suna amfani da node ID ɗinsu da aka ƙirƙira, don haka
UUID yana zama ɓangare na sunan canjin. Ƙimar ita ce tabbatacciyar cikakkiyar lamba ta tokens; duk wani abu
dabam (fanko, `0`, mara kyau, wanda ba lamba ba) ana yin watsi da shi, sannan warwarewar ta koma mataki na gaba.

| ID na Mai Bayarwa                                           | Sunan variable                                                             |
| ----------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| agentrouter                                                 | CONTEXT_LENGTH_AGENTROUTER                                                 |
| openrouter                                                  | CONTEXT_LENGTH_OPENROUTER                                                  |
| openai-compatible-chat-0f8e2c1a-5b7d-4e3f-9a6c-2d1b8e4f7a90 | CONTEXT_LENGTH_OPENAI_COMPATIBLE_CHAT_0F8E2C1A_5B7D_4E3F_9A6C_2D1B8E4F7A90 |

**Fifiko.** Ga kowane haɗin `provider/model`, mataki na farko da ya samar da ƙima shi ne zai yi aiki:

1. `CONTEXT_LENGTH_<PROVIDER>` na wannan mai bayarwa.
2. `CONTEXT_LENGTH_DEFAULT` (sauyin yanayi na gama-gari).
3. Tagar kowane model: sauyin context da aka adana (wanda aka saita a dashboard, ko kuma mai daidaita ganowa ya kafe ta atomatik, duba `CONTEXT_WINDOW_RECONCILE_INTERVAL`), in ba haka ba ƙimar catalog / models.dev da aka daidaita.
4. `defaultContextLength` na registry na mai bayarwar.
5. Hasashen sunan model (`claude` 200000, `gemini` 1000000, `gpt` / `o1` / `o3` / `o4` /
   `codex` 400000).
6. Tsoffin ƙimomi da aka gina a ciki na kowane mai bayarwa, sannan `128000`.

Duk matakan yanayi biyu suna aiki ne ga **duk mai bayarwar**: kowane model na wannan mai bayarwar zai samu iyaka iri ɗaya, kuma suna da fifiko a kan sauye-sauyen kowane model na dashboard. Idan model ɗaya kawai na wani mai bayarwa ke buƙatar wata taga ta daban, yi amfani da sauyin kowane model a dashboard maimakon haka.

**Iyaka.** Wannan jerin yana tafiyar da binciken tagar context a lokacin request (ƙin amincewar
`context_length_exceeded` / "Input exceeds context window" a cikin
`open-sse/handlers/chatCore.ts`), kasafin matsawar prompt da iyakokin context na combo. `context_length` da `GET /v1/models` ke sanarwa har yanzu yana iya fitowa daga metadata na catalog ko registry, kuma ba a tabbatar cewa zai nuna sauyin yanayi ba.

**Misali.** Mai bayarwa wanda model ɗinsa ke karɓar tagar token 1,050,000 yayin da catalog ke nuna
128,000:

```dotenv
CONTEXT_LENGTH_AGENTROUTER=1050000
```

Sake kunna server bayan canza shi: ana karanta ƙimar ne daga yanayin process na server. A kan **manhajar desktop**, server yana gudana a matsayin child process kuma ba ya ganin system variable da aka saita bayan an ƙaddamar da manhajar; saka layin a cikin fayil ɗin `.env` da manhajar ke lodawa (jerin bincike a
[Jagorar Electron: Binciken fayil ɗin yanayi](../guides/ELECTRON_GUIDE.md#environment-file-lookup)),
sannan ka rufe manhajar gaba ɗaya ka sake ƙaddamar da ita.

### Matsawa

| Variable                              | Tsoho         | Bayani                                                                                                                                     |
| ------------------------------------- | ------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `OMNIROUTE_RTK_TRUST_PROJECT_FILTERS` | ba a saita ba | Amince da `.rtk/filters.json` na project ba tare da hash na `.rtk/trust.json` ba. Yi amfani da shi kawai a ci gaban gida da ake sarrafawa. |

### Injin Memory (shiri na 21)

### Kuɗin event-loop na memory, skills, da sabunta token (#10349)

OmniRoute **process ɗin Node guda ɗaya ne**. Ciro/dawo da memory, shigar da skills, da sabunta token na mai bayarwa suna gudana a kan **event loop ɗin nan ɗaya** da `GET /healthz` da dashboard. Ba worker thread ba ne.

| Aiki                  | Code                               | Tsoho                                               | Ikon mai gudanarwa                                                                                                                                   |
| --------------------- | ---------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Ciro / dawo da memory | `src/lib/memory/`                  | **memoryEnabled** na dashboard (a kunne ta tsohuwa) | Kashe **Settings → Memory**. Babu wani kill switch na env daban bayan kashe fasalin a cikin settings.                                                |
| Shigar da skills      | `src/lib/skills/injection.ts`      | **skillsEnabled** na dashboard (a kunne ta tsohuwa) | Kashe **Settings → Memory/Skills** (`skillsEnabled`). Saitunan sandbox da ke ƙasa suna iyakance aiwatarwa ne kawai bayan an riga an kunna shigarwar. |
| Sabunta token         | `src/sse/services/tokenRefresh.ts` | A kunne ga masu bayarwar OAuth/web da aka haɗa      | Cire haɗin mai bayarwar ko bar tokens su ci gaba da kasancewa masu aiki; babu env na `TOKEN_REFRESH=0` a yanzu.                                      |

Idan `/healthz` yana jinkiri a kan na'ura marar wani aiki, fara da kashe memory + skills, sannan bincika nauyin catalog/compression (#10303, #9685). Waɗannan fasaloli suna ba da dama a wuraren `await`, amma har yanzu suna gogayya kan thread guda ɗin.

Saitunan embedding layer, vector store da reranking na persistent memory subsystem (`src/lib/memory/`).

| Sauyi                                | Tsoho                       | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| ------------------------------------ | --------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `MEMORY_EMBEDDING_CACHE_TTL_MS`      | `300000` (minti 5)          | TTL na ma’ajiyar embedding ta cikin ƙwaƙwalwa (ga kowane sa hannun tushe/model/girma).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `MEMORY_EMBEDDING_CACHE_MAX`         | `1000`                      | Matsakaicin adadin shigarwar LRU da ake riƙewa a ma’ajiyar embedding.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `MEMORY_TRANSFORMERS_MODEL`          | `Xenova/all-MiniLM-L6-v2`   | ID na ma’ajiyar HF don pipeline na MiniLM na gida na `@huggingface/transformers` da ake kunna bisa zaɓi (~23 MB int8, ~400 MB RAM).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `MEMORY_STATIC_MODEL`                | `minishlab/potion-base-8M`  | ID na ma’ajiyar HF don mai-embedding na teburin bincike na static potion/Model2Vec. Ana sauke shi a hankali zuwa kundin ma’ajiya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `MEMORY_STATIC_CACHE_DIR`            | `<DATA_DIR>/embeddings`     | Kundin da ake amfani da shi don adana fayilolin model na static potion. Idan ba a saita shi ba, tsohonsa yana ƙarƙashin `DATA_DIR`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `HF_HUB_ENDPOINT`                    | `https://huggingface.co`    | Sauya tushen URL na Hugging Face Hub da `staticPotion.ts` ke amfani da shi (misali, endpoint na madubi don saitunan da ba su da haɗin intanet).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `MEMORY_VEC_TOP_K`                   | `20`                        | Tsohon top-K da binciken vector na brute-force na `sqlite-vec` ke amfani da shi a cikin `src/lib/memory/vectorStore.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `MEMORY_RRF_K`                       | `60`                        | Ƙimar dindindin `k` ta Reciprocal Rank Fusion don haɗaɗɗen dawo da bayanai na FTS5 + vector (tsarin sqlite-vec).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `VECTOR_STORE_DISABLE_VEC`           | `false`                     | Wurin gwaji/bincike a cikin `getVectorStore()` (`src/lib/memory/vectorStore.ts`): idan `true`, yana tilasta ma’ajiyar vector ta zama `null` (yana kwaikwayon muhallin cloud/WASM marar `sqlite-vec`), yana rage dawo da ƙwaƙwalwa zuwa binciken kalmomin FTS5. A bar shi ba a saita ba a production.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `NOTION_API_KEY`                     | _(ba a saita ba)_           | Maɓallin API na backend na Notion (wanda saitin backend da aka sani na `genericBackend.ts` ke amfani da shi).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `NOTION_API_URL`                     | `https://api.notion.com/v1` | Tushen URL na API na Notion (ana iya sauya shi don madadin Notion da ake ɗaukar nauyinsa da kai).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OBSIDIAN_API_KEY`                   | _(ba a saita ba)_           | Maɓallin API na backend na Obsidian Vault (wanda saitin backend da aka sani na `genericBackend.ts` ke amfani da shi).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OBSIDIAN_API_URL`                   | `http://localhost:27123`    | Tushen URL na API na Obsidian Vault (ana iya sauya shi don vault na nesa).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_ENABLED`         | `false`                     | Babban maɓallin lalacewar ƙwaƙwalwa bisa nau’i na TV6. **Ana kunnawa bisa zaɓi (a kashe yake da farko)** — sharewar tana **goge** ƙwaƙwalwar da ta lalace. Idan yana kashe, `access_count`/`last_accessed_at` bayanan sa-ido ne kawai kuma ba a taɓa goge komai ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_EPISODIC_DAYS`   | `30`                        | TTL (kwanaki) wanda bayan sa ƙwaƙwalwar `episodic` da ba a yi amfani da ita ba za ta lalace. `0` yana sa episodic ma ta zama kariyayyiya. Nau’ukan masu ɗorewa (`factual`/`procedural`/`semantic`) koyaushe suna da kariya. Agogon lalacewar yana sake farawa bisa `last_accessed_at`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_ACCESS_IMMUNITY` | `3`                         | Ƙwaƙwalwar da aka saka sau `>=` wannan adadi tana samun kariya daga lalacewa ba tare da la’akari da nau’inta ba. `0` yana kashe kariyar samun dama.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_SWEEP_INTERVAL`  | `0` (a kashe)               | Tazarar lokaci (daƙiƙoƙi) don sharewar lalacewa ta lokaci-lokaci ta zaɓi a cikin `src/lib/memory/typedDecay.ts`. `0`/ba a saita ba = babu sharewar lokaci-lokaci. Ana buƙatar kunnawa sau biyu: dole kuma `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_ENABLED=true`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_STRICT_SYSTEM_PROVIDERS`  | _(ba a saita ba)_           | ID na providers da aka raba da waƙafi (ba a bambanta manya da ƙananan haruffa ba) waɗanda ke karɓar saƙon `system` **kawai a index 0** (`src/lib/memory/injection.ts`). Ga waɗannan, saka ƙwaƙwalwa a tsakiyar jerin cikin hanyar da ta dace da cache ba shi da aminci a tattaunawar zagaye da yawa, don haka ana haɗa/ƙara ƙwaƙwalwar a gaba a matsayin saƙon system na farko. Tsoho ya ƙunshi `xiaomi-mimo`/`mimo` kawai; faɗaɗa shi don endpoints masu dacewa da OpenAI da ake ɗaukar nauyinsu da kai (misali Qwen3.5+/3.6) waɗanda chat template ɗinsu ke tilasta irin wannan ƙa’idar saƙon system guda ɗaya a gaba. |

### Misalin Docker Mai Ƙarancin RAM

`128` na dashboard kawai ne. Coding agents a kan wannan heap suna samun `FATAL ERROR` yayin dogon `/v1/responses`. Kada a yi amfani da wannan misalin a matsayin gateway na Claude/Codex/Grok.

```bash
OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB=128
PROMPT_CACHE_MAX_SIZE=20
PROMPT_CACHE_MAX_BYTES=524288        # 512 KB
SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_SIZE=25
SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_BYTES=1048576     # 1 MB
STREAM_HISTORY_MAX=10
```

## Daidaita Arena ELO

| Sauyi                        | Tsoho         | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                                                      | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| ---------------------------- | ------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `ARENA_ELO_SYNC_ENABLED`     | `true`        | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts`                                     | Daidaita ELO na jadawalin jagororin Arena AI lokaci-lokaci, wanda za a iya saita shi daga Tutocin Fasali na Dashboard ko amfani da `false` don ƙin shiga.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `MODELS_CATALOG_PREFIX_MODE` | `dual`        | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts`, `src/app/api/v1/models/catalog.ts` | Tsarin prefix da ake amfani da shi ga ids na samfura a cikin `GET /v1/models`. `dual` yana nuna duka gajeren alias prefix da canonical provider prefix ga kowane samfuri (dacewa da sigogin baya — yana kusan ninka girman kasidar); `alias` yana fitar da id ɗaya ga kowane samfuri; `canonical` yana fitar da cikakken provider-id prefix kawai (masu samarwa waɗanda alias ɗinsu ya riga ya zama canonical id suna riƙe shigarwa ɗaya tak). Abokan hulɗa za su iya sauya wannan ga kowace buƙata ta amfani da `?prefix=alias`. Duba [API_REFERENCE](API_REFERENCE.md#model-id-prefixes-prefix). |
| `ARENA_ELO_SYNC_INTERVAL`    | `86400` (24h) | `src/lib/arenaEloSync.ts`                                                            | Tsakanin lokacin daidaitawa a cikin daƙiƙu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |

## Mai Samar da Playground na PromptQL (Ba na Hukuma ba/Na Gwaji)

Gadar zaman GraphQL da aka sake gina ta hanyar nazarin tsarin prompt.ql.app (`src/shared/constants/providers/web-cookie.ts`). Dukansu na zaɓi ne — saitunan tsoho suna nuni zuwa endpoints na playground na jama'a; a sauya su kawai don tura PromptQL mai zaman kansa/wani dabam.

| Sauyi                        | Tsoho                                                              | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                                         | Bayani                                                                        |
| ---------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `PROMPTQL_GRAPHQL_ENDPOINT`  | `https://data.prompt.ql.app/promptql/playground-v2-hge/v1/graphql` | `open-sse/executors/promptql.ts`                                        | GraphQL endpoint da ake amfani da shi don ayyukan taɗi/zama.                  |
| `PROMPTQL_CREDITS_ENDPOINT`  | `https://data.pro.ql.app/v1/graphql`                               | `open-sse/executors/promptql.ts`, `open-sse/services/usage/promptql.ts` | GraphQL endpoint da ake amfani da shi don neman ma'aunin kuɗin amfani/amfani. |
| `PROMPTQL_TOKEN_REFRESH_URL` | `https://auth.pro.ql.app/ddn/project/token`                        | `open-sse/executors/promptql.ts`                                        | Endpoint da ake amfani da shi don sabunta token gwargwadon iyawa.             |
| `PROMPTQL_POLL_TIMEOUT_MS`   | `180000`                                                           | `open-sse/executors/promptql.ts`                                        | Matsakaicin lokaci (ms) na binciken `thread_events` kafin lokaci ya ƙare.     |

## Mai Bayar da HyperAgent na Yanar Gizo (Ba na Hukuma ba/Gwaji)

Gadar zama da aka gano ta hanyar nazarin baya don hyperagent.com (`src/shared/constants/providers/web-cookie.ts`). Na zaɓi ne — ƙimar tsohuwa tana nuni zuwa maƙurar biyan kuɗi/amfani ta jama'a; a sauya ta kawai don turawar HyperAgent da aka ɗauki nauyinta da kai ko wata madadiya.

| ---------------------- | --------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------- |

| -------------- | --------------------- | ------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `KILO_API_URL` | `https://api.kilo.ai` | `open-sse/services/usage/kilocode.ts` | Tushen URL da ake amfani da shi don ɗauko ma'aunin Kilo Code na mutum da amfanin Kilo Pass. |

## Mai Bayar da Adobe Firefly na Yanar Gizo (Ba na Hukuma ba/Gwaji)

Sabunta zama ta hanyar burauza don mai bayar da Adobe Firefly na yanar gizo
(`open-sse/services/adobeFireflyBrowserLogin.ts`, `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`,
`open-sse/services/adobeFireflyClient.ts`). Na zaɓi ne — an daidaita duk ƙimomin tsohuwa don shigarwar
kwamfutar tebur ta yau da kullum.

> **An cire a #9255.** Tsohon tsarin tafiyar Chrome mai haɗin CDP (adobeFireflyChromeRuntime.ts) an
> maye gurbinsa da sabis ɗin shiga ta burauza na Playwright, kuma saitunansa ba sa samuwa. Sauye-sauyen
> ADOBE_FIREFLY_CHROME_ CDP_PORT / VISIBLE / HEADED / PING / FORCE_RESTART, tare da
> ADOBE_FIREFLY_LOGIN_WAIT_MS da ADOBE_FIREFLY_FORTER_WAIT_MS, ba a karanta su a ko'ina cikin
> tushen lambar — saita su ba ya yin wani tasiri.

| Sauyi                                | Ƙimar Tsohuwa     | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                 | Bayani                                                                                                                                    |
| ------------------------------------ | ----------------- | ----------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_CHROME_HEADLESS`      | `0`               | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflyBrowserLogin.ts` | Saita zuwa `1` don Chrome marar fuskar gani na gaske (an san cewa ba ya aiki don ƙirƙirawa; don gano matsala kawai).                      |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BROWSER_REFRESH`      | `1`               | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`      | Shiga/fita daga dumama burauza tun da wuri. `0` yana kashe dumama tun da wuri (farfaɗowa daga 408 a tsakiyar rukuni har yanzu yana aiki). |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_SESSION_DISK`         | `1`               | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`      | Saita zuwa `0` don kashe adana zaman Adobe Firefly a faifai.                                                                              |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_MIN_SUBMIT_GAP_MS`    | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`      | Mafi ƙarancin tazara (ms) da ake tilastawa tsakanin aika buƙatu a jere, yana maye gurbin ƙimar tsohuwa da aka gina a ciki.                |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BATCH_EXTRA_GAP_MS`   | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`      | Ƙarin tazara (ms) da ake ƙara bayan nasarar rukuni, yana maye gurbin ƙimar tsohuwa da aka gina a ciki.                                    |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_SUBMIT_BASE_DELAY_MS` | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflyClient.ts`       | Jinkiri na tushe (ms) kafin aika buƙatar ƙirƙirawa, yana maye gurbin ƙimar tsohuwa da aka gina a ciki.                                    |

## 19. Daidaita Model (Dev)

| Maɓalli                             | Tsoho             | Fayil ɗin Tushe                    | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| ----------------------------------- | ----------------- | ---------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `MODELS_DEV_SYNC_ENABLED`           | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `src/lib/modelsDevSync.ts`         | Tilasta kunna ko kashe daidaita farashin models.dev. Idan ba a saita ba = a bi Saituna > AI (`modelsDevSyncEnabled`). `0`/`false`/`off`/`no` **yana rinjayar DB** kuma yana tsallake daidaitawa ta lokaci-lokaci da binciken SQL/JSON na `getModelsDevPricing()` (don farfaɗowa idan dashboard ya maƙale a kan event loop ɗaya). `1`/`true`/`on`/`yes` yana tilasta kunna daidaitawa. Ajiye/share farashi har yanzu suna kiran `backupDbFile("pre-write")`, wanda ba ya yin komai ƙarƙashin takurawar mintuna 60 ko `DISABLE_SQLITE_AUTO_BACKUP`. |
| `MODELS_DEV_SYNC_INTERVAL`          | `86400` (24h)     | `src/lib/modelsDevSync.ts`         | Tazarar daidaita kundin model a lokacin haɓakawa, cikin daƙiƙu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CONTEXT_WINDOW_RECONCILE_INTERVAL` | `86400` (24h)     | `src/lib/contextWindowResolver.ts` | Tazara (daƙiƙu) ga mai daidaita context-window mai gyara kansa (5004): yana liƙa windows da provider ya ayyana daga gano `/models` a matsayin sauye-sauyen `auto:discovery` idan sun bambanta da kundin. Saita zuwa `0` don kashewa. Yana sake amfani da bayanan da aka riga aka daidaita (ba sabon fetch); ba ya taɓa maye gurbin sauye-sauyen `manual`.                                                                                                                                                                                         |
