| ---------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |

| `MODEL_CATALOG_INCLUDE_NAMES`                  | `true`                                 | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts`                                   | Haɗa filayen `name` masu sauƙin nunawa a cikin amsoshin `/v1/models`. `ENABLE_CC_COMPATIBLE_PROVIDER` na relays na ɓangare na uku ne kawai waɗanda ke karɓar abokan hulɗar Claude Code
kaɗai. OmniRoute yana sake rubuta buƙatu domin waɗannan relays su karɓe su. Idan kana son amfani da
Claude Code CLI kawai, ko kuma ba ka da tabbacin abin da waɗannan relays suke, ka bar wannan a kashe sannan ka ƙara mai samarwa na yau da kullum
wanda ya dace da Anthropic a maimakonsa.

| `PROXY_HEALTH_INTERVAL_MS`                        | `600000`                    | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                              | Tazarar zagayen mai tsara binciken lafiya na bayan fage a cikin ms (mafi ƙanƙanta `60000`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_RECOVERY_INTERVAL_MS`               | `600000`                    | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                              | Tazarar zagayen dawo da lafiya a bayan-fage cikin ms: yawan lokacin da ake sake gwada proxies waɗanda a baya ba su da lafiya domin proxy da ya murmure ya koma zagayawa ba tare da sake kunnawa ba. Ƙimomin da ke ƙasa da `60000` za su koma ga ƙimar tsoho.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |

| `PROXY_HEALTH_TEST_CONCURRENCY`                   | `10`                        | `src/lib/proxyHealth/probeTarget.ts`                                                            | Adadin gwaje-gwajen da ake farawa lokaci guda a kowace rukuni, wanda mai tsara lokaci da maƙurar `/api/settings/proxies/auto-test` suke amfani da shi tare. | `PROXY_HEALTH_TEST_STAGGER_MS`                    | `100`                       | `src/lib/proxyHealth/probeTarget.ts`                                                            | Jinkiri cikin ms tsakanin tashi biyu na gwaji a cikin rukuni. | `PROXY_HEALTH_AUTO_DEACTIVATE`                    | `false`                     | `src/lib/proxyHealth/statusPolicy.ts`                                                           | Lokacin da yake `false` (tsoho), gwaje-gwajen isa na atomatik (mai tsara lokaci + maɓallin "Gwada Duka" na `/api/settings/proxies/auto-test`) na **karantawa kaɗai** ne kuma ba sa taɓa rubuta matsayin proxy — mai gudanarwa kaɗai ne ke saita aiki/rashin aiki, don haka gwaji marar tabbas ba zai iya barin proxy da aka ware a makale ba (#6246). Saita `true` don dawo da tsohon halin gwadawa-da-saitawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `FLUSH_EMPTY_RETRY_ENABLED`                       | `false`                     | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`                                                              | Alamar fasali ta zaɓin shiga (duba [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md); maye-gurbin DB na dashboard ne ke da rinjaye). `true` (ko `1`, `yes`) yana sake gwada zagayen streaming da aka fassara wanda babu komai a cikinsa ta hanyar bayanan shaida ta al'ada (har zuwa sake-gwaje `STREAM_RECOVERY.EMPTY_TURN_RETRY_MAX`) maimakon nuna 200 mara komai ko 502 mai abun ciki mara komai.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `PROXY_POOL_EGRESS_OBSERVATION`                   | `false`                     | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`                                                              | Alamar fasali ta zaɓin shiga (duba [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md); maye-gurbin DB na dashboard ne ke da rinjaye). `true` (ko `1`, `yes`) yana nuna lura da fitowar pool ta karantawa kaɗai a ƙarƙashin proxy pool a dashboard (IP na fita daban-daban, haɗe-haɗe, da wanda aka fi gani a bayan IP guda ɗaya cikin awanni 24 da suka gabata, daga log ɗin proxy). Ba a taɓa amfani da shi wajen zaɓin hanya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `PROXY_OPERATOR_EGRESS_ENABLED`                   | `false`                     | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`                                                              | Tutar fasalin zaɓin shiga (duba [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md); saitin maye gurbi na DB na dashboard yana da rinjaye). `true` (ko `1`, `yes`) yana karɓar adiresoshin da aka gani masu kwanan wata da operator ya tura ga kowane memban pool, sannan ya haɗa su da karatun journal domin nunawa da tsara jerin pool.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `PROXY_OPERATOR_EGRESS_WINDOW_HOURS`              | `24`                        | `src/lib/db/proxyOperatorEgress.ts`                                                             | Tagar lura a cikin sa'o'i don layukan egress da operator ya tura (ana karantawa a kowane karatu; ba a buƙatar sake farawa). An iyakance ta zuwa 1..168; ƙimar da ta fita daga iyaka, wadda ba lamba ba ce, ko mara komai tana komawa 24 tare da gargaɗi guda ɗaya a faɗin process, ba tare da jefa kuskure ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE`                               | `false`                     | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                              | Saita `true` don bai wa scheduler damar cire proxies ta atomatik bayan gazawa a jere da ta maimaitu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE_AFTER`                         | `3`                         | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                              | Adadin gazawa a jere kafin scheduler ya cire proxy ta atomatik (lokacin da `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE=true`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `PROXY_AUTO_DISABLE`                              | `false`                     | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                              | Saita `true` don bai wa scheduler damar kashe proxy a sauƙaƙe (matsayi `dead`, ba za a taɓa gogewa ba) bayan gazawa a jere da ta maimaitu, maimakon cire ta. Wannan madadin `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE` ne da ba ya lalata bayanai: proxy ɗin tana fita daga warwarewar pool/rotation nan take (tacewar alive-status da warwarewar scope-pool ke amfani da ita tuni tana ware ta) kuma ana sake kunna ta ta atomatik da zarar ta fara cin gwajin probe kuma. Tana amfani da ma'aunin `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE_AFTER`. Idan tutocin biyu suna `true`, `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE` ce ke da rinjaye.                                                                                                                                       |

A kashe take ta tsohuwa: ana yi wa kowace proxy probe.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `PROXY_PASSIVE_WINDOW_MS`                         | `300000`                    | `src/lib/proxyHealth/passiveVerdict.ts`                                                         | Tagar zirga-zirgar production a cikin ms da tsallakewar passive ke dubawa (an iyakance `60000..599999`). Ta fi tazarar sweep ta minti 10 gajarta domin gazawar kwanan nan ta yi rinjaye maimakon a raunana tasirinta.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTROL_PLANE_PROXY_DIRECT_FALLBACK`   | `false`                     | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts`                                                | Ba OAuth da hanyoyin tabbatar da provider damar ƙetare pinned proxy su haɗu kai tsaye idan gwaje-gwajen farko na samuwar haɗi da proxy suka gaza. Tsarin fifikon da ake amfani da shi shi ne saitin maye gurbi na Feature Flags DB > env var > tsohon ƙima.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `RATE_LIMIT_MAX_WAIT_MS`                          | `30000` (30s)               | `src/lib/resilience/settings.ts`                                                                | Tsohon iyakar lokacin **jira-a-layi**: tsawon lokacin da buƙata za ta iya jiran gurbin mai samarwa kuma ta kasance a layi kafin a ƙi ta, ba tare da ta taɓa isa ga upstream ba. Ana goge mai ƙidayar lokaci da zarar aikin ya fara gudana — ana iyakance lokacin aiwatarwa daban ta `RATE_LIMIT_EXECUTION_MAX_WAIT_MS`. Env yana saita tsohuwar ƙima ne kawai: `resilienceSettings.requestQueue.maxWaitMs` da aka adana, sannan `rateLimitOverrides.maxWaitMs` na kowace haɗi, kowannensu yana rinjayar wannan ƙimar.                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `RATE_LIMIT_EXECUTION_MAX_WAIT_MS`                | `600000` (minti 10)         | `open-sse/services/rateLimitManager.ts`                                                         | Matsakaicin tsawon lokacin da buƙatar da aka amince da ita za ta iya ci gaba da aiwatarwa kafin ajiyar rate-limit ɗinta ta ƙare — an ware shi daga iyakar jira-a-layi domin jinkirin fara fetch a kan gateways marasa incremental kada ya haifar da timeout (#12027).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `RATE_LIMIT_MAX_QUEUE_DEPTH`                      | `0` (an kashe)              | `open-sse/services/rateLimitManager.ts`                                                         | Iyakar karɓar buƙatu cikin layi: a ƙi da 429 `queue_full` da zarar adadin buƙatun da ke layi ya kai wannan. `0` = babu iyaka (tsohuwar ƙima).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `RATE_LIMIT_AUTO_ENABLE`                          | _(ba a saita ba)_           | `open-sse/services/rateLimitManager.ts`                                                         | Tilasta kunna ko kashe kariyar rate limit ta auto-enable ba tare da la’akari da saitin Dashboard da aka adana ba. Yana karɓar `true`/`1`/`on` don tilasta kunnawa, `false`/`0`/`off` don tilasta kashewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `PROVIDER_COOLDOWN_ENABLED`                       | _(ba a saita ba → a kashe)_ | `open-sse/services/providerCooldownTracker.ts`                                                  | Bin diddigin cooldown na mai samarwa/haɗi na duniya a tsakanin buƙatu, wanda sai an zaɓi a kunna. A KASHE yake ta tsohuwa (yana cin karo da Connection Cooldown / Provider Circuit Breaker). Yana karɓar `true`/`1`/`on` don kunnawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `PROVIDER_COOLDOWN_MIN_MS`                        | `5000`                      | `open-sse/services/providerCooldownTracker.ts`                                                  | Mafi ƙarancin cooldown (ms) kafin a sake gwada mai samarwa/haɗin da ya gaza. Yana ƙaruwa da sauri bisa yawan gazawar da suka biyo juna. Ana amfani da shi ne kawai lokacin da aka kunna `PROVIDER_COOLDOWN_ENABLED`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `PROVIDER_COOLDOWN_MAX_MS`                        | `300000` (minti 5)          | `open-sse/services/providerCooldownTracker.ts`                                                  | Iyakar mafi girman cooldown (ms) kafin a sake gwada mai samarwa/haɗin da ya gaza ko da menene. Ana amfani da shi ne kawai lokacin da aka kunna `PROVIDER_COOLDOWN_ENABLED`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `STREAM_RECOVERY_ENABLED`                         | _(ba a saita ba → a kashe)_ | `src/lib/resilience/settings.ts` (fara-saiti) → `open-sse/services/streamRecovery.ts` (dabaru)  | **Mene ne:** dawo da rafukan upstream da suka yanke ba tare da mai amfani ya sani ba (port na free-claude-code). Yana riƙe taga SSE ta buɗewa har zuwa `STREAM_RECOVERY.HOLDBACK_MS` (750 ms), ta yadda yankewar _pre-commit_ — wadda ke faruwa kafin wani byte ya isa ga abokin ciniki — za a sake buɗe ta kuma a sake gwadawa ba tare da an gani ba. **Lokacin kunnawa:** upstreams marasa tabbas/waɗanda suke yawan yankewa da 0-byte a farkon rafi; a bar shi A KASHE idan ba za ka iya jure ƙarin lokaci har zuwa 750 ms kafin token na farko ya bayyana a kowane rafi ba. Yana karɓar `true`/`1`/`on`. Yana fara saita saitin Resilience da za a adana; saitin Dashboard yana rinjaya da zarar an saita shi. |
| `STREAM_RECOVERY_MIDSTREAM_ENABLED`               | _(ba a saita ba → a kashe)_ | `src/lib/resilience/settings.ts` (ƙimar farko) → `open-sse/services/streamRecovery.ts` (dabaru) | **Menene:** ci gaba daga tsakiyar stream (Fase 4.4) — bayan yankewar _post-commit_ (bytes sun riga sun isa ga abokin ciniki), a sake yin buƙata tare da rubutun da bai cika ba a matsayin assistant prefill sannan a haɗa ƙarshen da ya ɓace. Don streams masu bayyana rubutu kawai da suka dace da OpenAI; ba ya taɓa farawa yayin da ake gudanar da tool call. **Lokacin kunnawa:** dogayen samar da amsoshi da ake yankewa a tsakiyar amsa, kuma kana yarda ƙarshen da aka dawo da shi ya iso a dunƙule guda maimakon token-bi-token. Mai zaman kansa ne daga `STREAM_RECOVERY_ENABLED` (bambancin matakin haɗari). Yana karɓar `true`/`1`/`on`.                                                                |
| `STREAM_THROUGHPUT_WATCHDOG_ENABLED`              | _(ba a saita ba → a kashe)_ | `src/lib/resilience/settings.ts` → `open-sse/services/throughputWatchdog.ts`                    | Na’urar sa ido ta fitarwa mai amfani ta active-stream wadda sai an zaɓi kunna ta. Tana gano streams da ke ci gaba da aika chunks amma suka kasance ƙasa da ƙimar fitarwar assistant da aka saita; heartbeats, usage events, empty deltas, da matakan tool/reasoning ba sa yin kama da ci gaba. Ta bambanta da lokutan ƙarewar rashin aiki da na wa’adin ƙarshe.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `STREAM_THROUGHPUT_WATCHDOG_WARMUP_MS`            | `30000`                     | `src/lib/resilience/settings/normalize.ts`                                                      | Lokacin rangwame kafin a tantance throughput, an iyakance shi zuwa 0–600000 ms.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `STREAM_THROUGHPUT_WATCHDOG_WINDOW_MS`            | `30000`                     | `src/lib/resilience/settings/normalize.ts`                                                      | Tagar fitarwa mai amfani mai motsi, an iyakance ta zuwa 1000–600000 ms; ana buƙatar cikakkiyar taga guda kafin dakatarwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `STREAM_THROUGHPUT_WATCHDOG_MIN_BYTES_PER_SECOND` | `4`                         | `src/lib/resilience/settings/normalize.ts`                                                      | Mafi ƙarancin ƙimar bytes na fitarwar assistant ta UTF-8 (wakilin token mai taka-tsantsan), an iyakance ta zuwa 1–1000000.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `STREAM_THROUGHPUT_WATCHDOG_MIN_USEFUL_BYTES`     | `1`                         | `src/lib/resilience/settings/normalize.ts`                                                      | Mafi ƙarancin samfurin fitarwa mai amfani da ba sifili ba da ake ɗauka a matsayin abin aunawa, an iyakance shi zuwa bytes 1–1000000.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `HEALTHCHECK_STAGGER_MS`                          | `3000`                      | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`                                                                   | Tsakanin lokacin jinkirtawa (ms) tsakanin binciken lafiyar token na masu samarwa yayin farawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `HEALTHCHECK_JITTER_MIN_MS`                       | `500`                       | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`                                                                   | Mafi ƙarancin jitter na bazuwar (ms) da ake ƙarawa a kan `HEALTHCHECK_STAGGER_MS` tsakanin binciken lafiyar token na masu samarwa, don hana taruwar buƙatu lokaci guda (Issue #1220).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `HEALTHCHECK_JITTER_MAX_MS`                       | `5000`                      | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`                                                                   | Mafi girman jitter na bazuwar (ms) da ake ƙarawa a kan `HEALTHCHECK_STAGGER_MS` tsakanin binciken lafiyar token na masu samarwa, don hana taruwar buƙatu lokaci guda (Issue #1220).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `HEALTHCHECK_BATCH_SIZE`                          | `20`                        | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`                                                                   | Girman rukunin binciken lokaci guda don zagayen binciken lafiyar token na farawa; manyan ƙimomi suna bincika ƙarin haɗe-haɗe a lokaci guda, ƙananan ƙimomi kuma suna rage nauyin taruwar buƙatu (Issue #7875, koma-bayan #7719).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `REQUEST_RETRY`                                   | `2`                         | `src/sse/services/cooldownAwareRetry.ts`                                                        | Yawan sake-gwada ta atomatik kan martanin cooldown da ya shafi model kafin mayar da kuskure ga abokin ciniki.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `MAX_RETRY_INTERVAL_SEC`                          | `30`                        | `src/sse/services/cooldownAwareRetry.ts`                                                        | Matsakaicin tazarar jinkirin sake-gwaji (daƙiƙoƙi) tsakanin sake-gwaje bayan lokacin jira. Wannan ƙimar ce iyaka ba tare da la’akari da `Retry-After` na uwar-garke ta sama ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `HEADROOM_URL`                                    | `http://localhost:8787`     | `src/lib/headroom/detect.ts`                                                                    | URL na proxy na Headroom mai rage amfani da token. Tsarin rayuwar dashboard (`api/headroom/*`) yana ƙaddamar da CLI na `headroom-ai` na gida a kan loopback ta tsohuwa; a sauya wannan ne kawai domin nuna wani proxy na waje na Docker sidecar.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |

### Konstantan daidaita dawo da stream

Ana daidaita halayyar jinkirta dawo da stream ta hanyar konstantan da aka saka kai tsaye a cikin
`open-sse/config/constants.ts` (`STREAM_RECOVERY`), waɗanda aka nuna a nan don tunani —
canza su yana buƙatar gyaran lamba, ba env var ba:

- `STREAM_RECOVERY.HOLDBACK_MS = 750` — tsawon lokacin da ake riƙe taga ta farko ta SSE
  domin a iya sake gwada yankewar da ta faru da wuri kafin a tura ko byte ɗaya zuwa ga abokin ciniki.
- `STREAM_RECOVERY.BUFFER_MAX_BYTES = 65536` — ƙayyadadden iyakar taga da ake riƙewa; a tura
  (flush + passthrough) da zarar adadin byte ya kai wannan, ba tare da la’akari da ma’aunin lokaci ba.
- `STREAM_RECOVERY.EARLY_RETRY_MAX = 4` — matsakaicin adadin sake buɗe stream na uwar-garke
  ta sama a ɓoye yayin da har yanzu ba a tura abin da ake riƙewa ba.

> **Iyakar ƙimar sliding-window ta kowane mai samarwa (babu env var):** madadin
> iyakar ƙimar sliding-window ta kowane mai samarwa da aka ɗauko daga FCC tana cikin lamba
> (`open-sse/services/providerDefaultRateLimit.ts`, an haɗa ta ta hanyar
> `open-sse/services/rateLimitManager.ts`) amma tana zuwa da **taswirar tsoho mara komai**
> kuma **ba ta da env var na mai gudanarwa** a halin yanzu — ana kunna ta ne kawai ta hanyar test hook /
> gyaran lamba. Da gangan ba a jera ta a teburin da ke sama ba. Mai iyakance relay na kowane `(token, IP)`
> wanda _yake_ da abin da za a iya daidaitawa shi ne `RELAY_IP_PER_MINUTE` (§3 Network & Ports).

> [!CAUTION]
> Waɗannan variables suna samar da **cikakken output** kuma suna iya fallasa bayanai masu muhimmanci. **Kada a taɓa kunna su a production.**

| Variable                              | Tsoho                        | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                                                                       | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| ------------------------------------- | ---------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CURSOR_DEBUG`                        | _(ba a saita ba)_            | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Saita `1` don kunna cikakkun logs na Cursor executor (SSE chunks da aka decode, da sauransu).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `CURSOR_STREAM_DEBUG`                 | _(ba a saita ba)_            | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Alias mai dacewa da tsofaffin nau'ikan `CURSOR_DEBUG`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `CURSOR_DUMP_FILE`                    | _(ba a saita ba)_            | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Path na fayil na zaɓi wanda ke karɓar ɗanyen Cursor chunks da aka decode yayin da `CURSOR_DEBUG=1`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `CURSOR_STREAM_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `300000`                     | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Lokacin jira kafin stream marar aiki ya ƙare (ms) ga Cursor executor.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `CURSOR_KV_GRACE_MS`                  | `2000`                       | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Ƙarin lokacin jira (ms) bayan composer kv_after_text soft terminator yayin da bytes suka rage a buffer — yana ba trailing exec_mcp tool call lokaci don kammala frame ɗinsa.                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CURSOR_TOOL_DIRECTIVE`               | a kunne (`!== "0"`)          | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Tool-commit directive da ke sa composer-2.5 ya yi tool calls cikin aminci. Saita `0` don kashewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_SYSTEM_INSTRUCTION_APPEND` | _(ba a saita ba)_            | `open-sse/translator/request/claude-to-openai.ts`, `open-sse/translator/response/openai-to-claude.ts` | Rubutun system prompt da operator ya ayyana wanda ake liƙawa ga system message BAYAN translation (post-translation injection), yana isa ga hanyoyin codex/Responses da `/v1/messages`. Haka kuma ana amfani da shi a matsayin directive prefix da ake cirewa daga echoed system preamble blocks. Bar shi ba a saita ba don kashewa. |
| `OMNIROUTE_STRIP_SYSTEM_PREAMBLE`     | `0` (a kashe)                | `open-sse/translator/response/openai-to-claude.ts`, `open-sse/utils/directivePreambleStripper.ts`     | Saita zuwa `1` don cire echoed system-prompt preamble blocks daga farkon openai→claude stream. A kashe yake ta tsohuwa — heuristics ɗin sun dace da tsarin rubutun turanci kuma suna canza response payload, saboda haka reply da ya fara da irin wannan sashe bisa halacci zai rasa shi.                                           |

| `CURSOR_STATE_DB_PATH`                | _(ana bincikawa)_            | `open-sse/utils/cursorVersionDetector.ts`                                                             | Sauya hanyar binciken state DB na Cursor IDE da ake amfani da ita don gano nau'in IDE.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `CURSOR_AGENT_CLI_VERSION`            | _(gano / kulle)_             | `open-sse/utils/cursorAgentCliVersion.ts`                                                             | Agent CLI build id (`YYYY.MM.DD-<hash>`) don `x-cursor-client-version: cli-…` a Agent Run.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |

## 23. Haɗin GitHub

Ba masu amfani damar kai rahoton matsaloli kai tsaye daga Dashboard.

| Sauyi                 | Tsoho             | Fayil ɗin Tushe                               | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                           |
| --------------------- | ----------------- | --------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `GITHUB_ISSUES_REPO`  | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `src/app/api/v1/issues/report/route.ts`       | Ma'ajiyar lamba a tsarin `owner/repo`.                                                                                                                                           |
| `GITHUB_ISSUES_TOKEN` | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `src/app/api/v1/issues/report/route.ts`       | GitHub Personal Access Token mai izinin `issues:write`.                                                                                                                          |
| `GITHUB_TOKEN`        | _(ba a saita ba)_ | tantance matsaloli / mataimakan wakilin cloud | Alamar samun damar GitHub ta gama-gari da ake amfani da ita a matsayin madadin `GITHUB_ISSUES_TOKEN`, kuma mataimakan wakilin cloud a `src/lib/cloudAgent/*` suna amfani da ita. |

## Yanayin Turawa

Don jagorar SRE ta backend na relay (halayen ts/bifrost/auto, wurin sanya 9router idan aka kwatanta da CLIProxyAPI, da dabarar komawa madadin yayin yawan zirga-zirga), duba [Dabarar Backend na Relay](/docs/reference/RELAY_BACKEND_STRATEGY.md).

### Mafi Ƙarancin Saitin Ci-gaban Gida

```bash
JWT_SECRET=$(openssl rand -base64 48)
API_KEY_SECRET=$(openssl rand -hex 32)
INITIAL_PASSWORD=dev123
PORT=20128
NODE_ENV=development
```

### Docker na Yanayin Samarwa

```bash
JWT_SECRET=<wanda-aka-ƙirƙira>
API_KEY_SECRET=<wanda-aka-ƙirƙira>
INITIAL_PASSWORD=<wanda-aka-ƙirƙira>
STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY=<wanda-aka-ƙirƙira>
DATA_DIR=/data
PORT=20128
API_PORT=20129
NODE_ENV=production
AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE=true
REQUIRE_API_KEY=true
NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL=https://omniroute.example.com
BASE_URL=http://localhost:20128
OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB=8192
CORS_ORIGIN=https://your-frontend.example.com
```

### Muhalli Marar Haɗin Intanet / CI

```bash
JWT_SECRET=test-jwt-secret-for-ci
API_KEY_SECRET=test-api-key-secret-for-ci
INITIAL_PASSWORD=testpass
NODE_ENV=production
OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_BACKGROUND_SERVICES=true
APP_LOG_TO_FILE=false
```

### VPS tare da Reverse Proxy (nginx + Cloudflare)

```bash
JWT_SECRET=<wanda-aka-ƙirƙira>
API_KEY_SECRET=<wanda-aka-ƙirƙira>
STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY=<wanda-aka-ƙirƙira>
PORT=20128
AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE=true
REQUIRE_API_KEY=true
NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL=https://omniroute.example.com
BASE_URL=http://127.0.0.1:20128
CORS_ORIGIN=https://omniroute.example.com
ENABLE_TLS_FINGERPRINT=true
CLI_COMPAT_ALL=1
```

## 24. Skills Sandbox (v3.8.0+)

Iyakoki da saitunan tsaro da ake amfani da su lokacin da tsarin Skills (`src/lib/skills/`) ke aiwatar da ayyukan atomatik da masu amfani suka ayyana a cikin muhalli mai keɓewa.

| Sauyi                             | Tsoho                                          | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                                    | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| --------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_TIMEOUT_MS`       | `10000` (10 s)                                 | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Iyakar lokacin agogo ga kowace aiwatarwar lambar skill a cikin sandbox. Iyaka ce tabbatacciya; ana kashe duk abin da ya wuce ta.                                                                                                                                                       |
| `SKILLS_EXECUTION_TIMEOUT_MS`     | _(yana komawa ga `SKILLS_SANDBOX_TIMEOUT_MS`)_ | `src/lib/skills/`                                                  | Iyakar lokacin tsara aiwatar da skill a babban mataki. Saita shi sama da `SKILLS_SANDBOX_TIMEOUT_MS` don ba da damar jerin ayyuka masu matakai da yawa.                                                                                                                                |
| `SKILLS_MAX_FILE_BYTES`           | `1048576` (1 MB)                               | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Matsakaicin bytes da skill zai iya karantawa daga kowane fayil guda ɗaya a cikin sandbox.                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `SKILLS_MAX_HTTP_RESPONSE_BYTES`  | `256000` (250 KB)                              | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Matsakaicin bytes da ake ɗauka daga kowace amsar HTTP guda ɗaya a cikin skill.                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `SKILLS_MAX_SANDBOX_OUTPUT_CHARS` | `100000`                                       | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Tabbatacciyar iyaka ga haruffan stdout/stderr da ake dawo da su daga kiran sandbox.                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_NETWORK_ENABLED`  | `false`                                        | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Saita `1`/`true` don ba da damar sadarwar fita daga cikin sandbox. Ta tsohuwa tana kasancewa **keɓantacciya** saboda tsaro.                                                                                                                                                            |
| `SKILLS_ALLOWED_SANDBOX_IMAGES`   | _(babu komai)_                                 | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Jerin container images da aka ware da waƙafi waɗanda aka amince da su don aiwatarwa a sandbox. Idan babu komai, ana amfani da tsohon ginannen zaɓi kawai.                                                                                                                              |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_DOCKER_IMAGE`     | _(tsohon ginannen zaɓi)_                       | `src/lib/skills/`                                                  | Container image da ake amfani da shi lokacin ƙirƙirar sandbox mai dogaro da Docker. Sauya shi don ɗaure amfani da keɓaɓɓen base image mai ƙarfafa tsaro.                                                                                                                               |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_RUNTIME`          | `auto`                                         | `src/lib/skills/sandbox.ts`, `src/lib/skills/containerProvider.ts` | Container runtime don keɓe skills: `auto` \| `docker` \| `apple` \| `wsl` \| `orbstack` \| `podman`. `auto` yana zaɓar runtime mafi dacewa da aka shigar bisa OS na host (Apple Container/OrbStack a macOS, WSL Container a Windows, Podman a Linux), sannan ya koma Docker idan babu. |

> [!CAUTION]
> Kunna `SKILLS_SANDBOX_NETWORK_ENABLED=true` yana buɗe hanyar fitar bayanai daga kowace lambar skill. Haɗa shi da `OUTBOUND_SSRF_GUARD_ENABLED=true` da tsauraran manufofin `CORS_ORIGIN`/proxy a turawar da ake rabawa.

## 25. Ƙayyadaddun Amfani na Masu Bayarwa, Ramukan Sadarwa, Ajiyar Bayanai & Sauran Abubuwan Lokacin Gudanarwa

Wuraren shiga na ƙayyadaddun amfani na masu bayarwa, ramukan cibiyar sadarwa (Tailscale, Ngrok, wakilin gyaran kurakurai na MITM), tafkin fitarwa na 1Proxy, ajiyar bayanan rumbun bayanai da ƙananan keɓance-keɓance na kowane fasali waɗanda matakin aiwatarwa ko rubutun umarni ke amfani da su.

| Sauyi                                          | Tsoho                                                                       | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                                           | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| ---------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `REDIS_URL`                                    | `redis://localhost:6379`                                                    | `src/shared/utils/rateLimiter.ts`                                         | Kirtanin haɗin Redis don tsarin bayan rate limiter.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `ALIBABA_CODING_PLAN_HOST`                     | _(uwar garken samarwa)_                                                     | `open-sse/services/bailianQuotaFetcher.ts`                                | Keɓance uwar garken da ake amfani da ita wajen ɗauko ƙayyadaddun amfani na tsarin coding na Alibaba Bailian.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `ALIBABA_CODING_PLAN_QUOTA_URL`                | an samo shi daga uwar garken                                                | `open-sse/services/bailianQuotaFetcher.ts`                                | Keɓance cikakken URL na ƙayyadaddun amfani na Alibaba Bailian.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `QWEN_CLOUD_COOKIE`                            | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                           | `open-sse/services/qwenTokenPlanQuotaFetcher.ts`                          | Cookie na zaman console don mashigar ƙayyadaddun amfani ta Token Plan na sirri a Qwen Cloud / Model Studio (maɓallin API na inference ba zai iya karanta shi ba). Kwafi gabaɗayan kan buƙatar `Cookie` — yana ɗauke da `login_qwencloud_ticket` — daga duk wani kiran `api.json` zuwa `cs-data.qwencloud.com` a home.qwencloud.com › Billing › Subscription (F12 › Network). Yana da matuƙar sirri kuma ya keɓanta ga zaman; an fi son filin Dashboard na `qwenCloudCookie` na kowane haɗi.                                                                                      |
| `QWEN_CLOUD_SEC_TOKEN`                         | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                           | `open-sse/services/qwenTokenPlanQuotaFetcher.ts`                          | Keɓance `sec_token` da hannu don mashigar console ta Token Plan. Yana da matuƙar sirri; idan ba a saita shi ba, mai ɗauko bayanan zai samo shi daga HTML na dashboard ta amfani da cookie.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |

Idan `false` ne, kurakuran `502` ba za su ƙara jawo sauya asusu ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_502_THRESHOLD`               | `1`                                                                         | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Adadin kurakuran `502` a cikin `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_502_WINDOW_SECONDS` da ake buƙata kafin a sauya asusun. `1` (tsoho) yana sauyawa nan take.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_502_WINDOW_SECONDS`          | `120`                                                                       | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Tagar lokaci mai motsi (daƙiƙoƙi) wadda a cikinta ake ƙirga kurakuran `502` zuwa `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_502_THRESHOLD`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_ON_400`                      | `false`                                                                     | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Zaɓin shiga (a tsohuwa KASHE): idan `true`, `400` na yau da kullum (buƙata mara kyau) shima yana jawo sauya asusu. Wannan ƙari ne kawai — ba ya taɓa hana halin injin da yake akwai inda `400` mai ɗauke da rubutun iyakar amfani/kason amfani yake ci gaba da sauya asusu ba tare da la’akari da wannan tutar ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_400_THRESHOLD`               | `1`                                                                         | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Adadin kurakuran `400` a cikin `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_400_WINDOW_SECONDS` da ake buƙata kafin a sauya asusun (ana duba wannan ne kawai idan `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_ON_400=true`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_400_WINDOW_SECONDS`          | `120`                                                                       | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Tagar lokaci mai motsi (daƙiƙoƙi) wadda ake ƙirga kurakuran `400` a cikinta zuwa ga `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_400_THRESHOLD`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

### Mai Tsara Dumama na Claude

Dumama da cron ke tafiyarwa don haɗin Anthropic OAuth da aka zaɓa, domin a buɗe tagar iyakar amfani ta awa 5 ta hanyar ƙaramar buƙata da aka tsara maimakon buƙata ta ainihi ta farko (#8848). Mai tsara yana kashe sai idan `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_ENABLED` yana da darajar gaskiya **kuma** an yi wa haɗin alama a cikin `settings.claudeWarmup.connections`; jerin haɗi mara komai yana nufin ba za a dumama komai ba ko da an kunna env var ɗin.

| Sauyi                          | Tsoho                       | Fayil ɗin Tushe              | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| ------------------------------ | --------------------------- | ---------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_ENABLED`     | _(ba a saita ba → kashe)_   | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Babban maɓallin kunna mai tsara dumama. Yana karɓar `1`/`true`/`yes`/`on` (ba tare da kula da manyan ko ƙananan haruffa ba, kuma an cire sarari). Duk wata daraja dabam, ko rashin saita shi, yana barin mai tsara a kashe. |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_CRON`        | `0 7 * * *`                 | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Bayanin cron mai filaye biyar don zagayen dumama, wanda ake tantancewa a cikin `America/Los_Angeles` (yankin lokacin sake saiti na Anthropic) ba tare da la’akari da agogon host ba.                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_CONCURRENCY` | `3`                         | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Adadin haɗin da ake dumamawa a lokaci guda a kowane zagaye. Ana taƙaita shi zuwa `1`-`10`; darajar da ba lamba ba tana komawa zuwa `3`.                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_MODEL`       | `claude-3-5-haiku-20241022` | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Model da ake amfani da shi don buƙatar dumama. Kada a sauya sai idan tsohon ba ya samuwa a tsarin ku; zaɓi model mafi arha wanda har yanzu zai buɗe tagar.                                                                  |

### Zaman VNC na Shiga ta Burauza & Laƙabin Data-Dir

Chromium+VNC da aka ƙunshe cikin container wanda ake amfani da shi don karɓar bayanan shaidar shiga ta burauza kai tsaye (`/api/vnc-session`), tare da tsohon laƙabin `DATA_DIR`. Dukkansu na zaɓi ne — tsoffin saitunan VNC suna nufin image ɗin `omniroute-vnc-chromium:local` da aka haɗa, kuma ana sauya su ne kawai don image na container na musamman, ports, ko daidaita tsarin rayuwa.

| Maɓalli                               | Tsoho                                                                    | Fayil ɗin Tushe                             | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| ------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IMAGE`                 | `omniroute-vnc-chromium:local`                                           | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Alamar Docker image don kwantenar shiga ta Chromium+VNC. Gina `docker/vnc-browser/chromium` ko saita wannan zuwa image na musamman.                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_DOCKER_BIN`                | `docker`                                                                 | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Fayil ɗin binary na tsarin tafiyar da kwantena da ake amfani da shi don ƙaddamar da kwantenar VNC (misali, saita shi zuwa `podman`).                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_VNC_PORT`    | `3000`                                                                   | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Tashar VNC/noVNC da aka buɗe a cikin kwantenar.                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_CDP_PORT`    | `9223`                                                                   | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Tashar Chrome DevTools Protocol a cikin kwantenar.                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_PROFILE_DIR` | `/config`                                                                | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Hanyar kundin bayanan martabar Chromium a cikin kwantenar.                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_PROFILE_DIR`           | `$HOME/.omniroute/browser-login-profiles`                                | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Kundin da ke kan na'urar mai masauki wanda ke riƙe da bayanan martabar shiga ta burauza da aka adana.                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IDLE_MS`               | `600000` (minti 10)                                                      | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Wa'adin rashin aiki (ms) kafin a cire zaman VNC da ba ya aiki.                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_MS`                | `1800000` (minti 30)                                                     | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Iyakar ƙarshe (ms) ta tsawon rayuwar zaman VNC guda ɗaya.                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_SESSIONS`          | `4`                                                                      | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Matsakaicin adadin zaman VNC da za su iya gudana a lokaci guda.                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_READY_MS`              | `45000`                                                                  | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Wa'adin jira (ms) har burauzar da ke cikin kwantena ta zama shirye don CDP.                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_HARVEST_MS`            | `20000`                                                                  | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Wa'adin (ms) tattara zaman da aka kama/kukis bayan an kammala shiga.                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CHROMIUM_ARGS`         | `--remote-debugging-port=9222 --no-first-run --no-default-browser-check` | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ƙarin tutocin layin umarni da ake miƙawa ga Chromium da ke cikin kwantena.                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_NETWORK`               | `omniroute-vnc-browser-login`                                            | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Keɓantacciyar hanyar sadarwar Docker da kwantenar shiga ta VNC ke haɗuwa da ita (#12571) maimakon gadar tsoho, don kada kwantenonin da ke maƙwabtaka su iya isa tashar gadar CDP ɗinta.                            |
| `VIBEPROXY_DATA_DIR`                  | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                        | `open-sse/services/notionThreadSessions.ts` | **Tsohon laƙabi** na `DATA_DIR`, wanda ake dubawa ne kawai bayan ba a saita `DATA_DIR` da `OMNIROUTE_DATA_DIR` ba. Yana gano ma'ajiyar zaman zaren yanar gizo na Notion (`<dir>/notion-web-thread-sessions.json`). |

## 26. Gwaji & Kayan Aikin E2E

Ana amfani da su ta `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`, `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs`,
`scripts/dev/run-ecosystem-tests.mjs`, da `scripts/build/uninstall.mjs`. A bar duk
ƙimomin da ke ƙasa ba tare da saita su ba a turawar samarwa.

| Sauyi                                  | Tsoho                             | Fayil ɗin Tushe                           | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| -------------------------------------- | --------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_E2E_BOOTSTRAP_MODE`         | `auth`                            | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Yanayin fara E2E (`auth`, `fresh`, `reuse`) don mai gudanar da Playwright.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_E2E_PASSWORD`               | yana komawa ga `INITIAL_PASSWORD` | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Kalmar sirrin mai gudanarwa da ake shigarwa cikin muhallin Playwright.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_LOCAL_HEALTHCHECK`  | `true`                            | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Kashe binciken lafiyar gida na lokaci-lokaci yayin gudanar da Playwright.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_TOKEN_HEALTHCHECK`  | `true`                            | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Kashe madaukin binciken lafiyar token na OAuth yayin gwaje-gwaje.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_HEALTHCHECK_SKIP_PROVIDERS` | _(ba a saita ba)_                 | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`             | Masu samarwa da aka raba da wakafi waɗanda aka cire daga binciken sabunta token na rigakafi (misali `codex,openai`). Madadin da aka keɓance na kashe binciken lafiya gaba ɗaya — masu samarwa masu gajeren TTL suna ci gaba da sabuntawa yayin da masu samarwa na cascade ke kasancewa masu amsawa kawai. |
| `OMNIROUTE_HIDE_HEALTHCHECK_LOGS`      | `true`                            | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Dakatar da hayaniyar bayanan binciken lafiya a stdout na Playwright.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_PLAYWRIGHT_SKIP_BUILD`      | `0`                               | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Tsallake ginin samarwa na Next.js kafin Playwright ya fara (ingantawa ta CI).                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_UNINSTALL_HOOK`        | `0`                               | `scripts/build/uninstall.mjs`             | Tsallake hook ɗin cire OmniRoute (CI na amfani da shi don kiyaye `node_modules` yadda yake).                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `ECOSYSTEM_SERVER_WAIT_MS`             | `180000`                          | `scripts/dev/run-ecosystem-tests.mjs`     | Lokacin jira (ms) har sai uwar garken ya kasance lafiya kafin gudanar da gwaje-gwajen tsarin muhalli/yarjejeniya.                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_URL`                   | `http://127.0.0.1:20128/login`    | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | URL da kayan aikin gwajin smoke na Electron ke tsammanin manhajar da aka shirya za ta samar.                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `45000`                           | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Jimillar iyakar lokaci (ms) kafin kayan aikin gwajin smoke ya daina jira.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_SETTLE_MS`             | `2000`                            | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Tagar jira (ms) bayan shafin ya loda.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_APP_EXECUTABLE`        | _(ta atomatik)_                   | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Takamaiman hanyar zuwa executable na Electron da aka shirya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_DATA_DIR`              | _(tmpdir)_                        | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Kundin bayanai don gudanar da gwajin smoke na Electron.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_KEEP_DATA`             | `0`                               | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Saita zuwa `1` don adana kundin adireshin bayanan gwajin smoke bayan an gama gudanarwar.                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_STREAM_LOGS`           | `0`                               | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Saita zuwa `1` don watsa rajistocin Electron zuwa stdout yayin gudanarwar.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_COLD_RESTART`          | `0`                               | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | #7592: sake ƙaddamarwa ta amfani da kundin adireshin bayanai ɗaya, sannan a tabbatar cewa ƙaddamarwa ta biyu ta zaɓi direban SQLite na asali.                                                                                                                                                             |
| `CLI_DEVIN_BIN`                        | _(binciken PATH)_                 | `open-sse/executors/devin-cli.ts`         | Sauya hanyar binary ta Devin CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |

### Bututun fassarar takardu

Ana amfani da shi ta `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs` (umarnin `npm run i18n:run`).
Dukkan masu canji biyar ba a saita su ta tsohuwa ba — saita su a cikin `.env` kawai a kan na'urorin
da ya kamata su iya gudanar da mai fassara takardun.

| Mai canji                           | Tsoho             | Fayil ɗin Tushe                    | Bayani                                                                             |
| ----------------------------------- | ----------------- | ---------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_API_URL`     | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs` | URL na tushe mai dacewa da OpenAI don tsarin bayan fassara.                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_API_KEY`     | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs` | Alamar Bearer don tsarin bayan fassara (ba a taɓa rubuta ta a rajista ba).         |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_MODEL`       | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs` | ID na samfurin, misali `gpt-4o-mini` ko `cx/gpt-5.4-mini`.                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_TIMEOUT_MS`  | `60000`           | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs` | Iyakar lokacin kowace buƙata a cikin milliseƙan.                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_CONCURRENCY` | `4`               | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs` | Buƙatun fassara masu gudana a layi ɗaya yayin aiki kan fayiloli / locales da yawa. |

## 27. Ciyarwar Radar (Karɓar Baƙunci da Kai)

Ƙarin zaɓi ne da tutar fasali ta RADAR_ENABLED ke sarrafawa (a kashe take ta tsohuwa — tutar
fasali ce da ake kunnawa ko kashewa ta hanyar Settings/DB, ba env var ba; duba
[docs/frameworks/RADAR.md](../frameworks/RADAR.md#flag-radar_enabled-default-off)).
Sauye-sauye huɗu na farko da ke ƙasa zaɓaɓɓun sauye-sauyen ƙimomi ne don ciyarwar da aka karɓi
baƙuncinta da kai ko aka yi mata fork da kuma hanyoyin supporter-key. Na biyar, `RADAR_ADMIN_URL`,
mahaɗi ne na daban wanda ba shi da ƙimar tsohuwa zuwa keɓaɓɓen kwamitin ayyuka na mai shi.
Duba [docs/frameworks/RADAR.md](../frameworks/RADAR.md) don cikakken bayanin
module da kuma
[jerin kunnawa daga farko har ƙarshe da saitin da ake jagoranta](../frameworks/RADAR.md#end-to-end-activation-and-guided-setup).

Ba a saita mai karanta sanarwar Home/Changelog na gama-gari ta hanyar environment
variable ba, kuma baya dogaro da tutar fasali ta RADAR_ENABLED. Yana karanta URL na `news.json`
na ma'ajiyar jama'a da aka ayyana a `src/shared/utils/releaseNotes.ts` ta hanyar
GET kawai; ID na sanarwar da aka yi watsi da su suna ci gaba da kasancewa a ma'ajiyar gida ta burauza.

| Sauyi                         | Ƙimar Tsohuwa                                | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                              | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| ----------------------------- | -------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `RADAR_FEED_URL`              | `https://radar.omniroute.online`             | `src/lib/radar/{sync,referralsSync,offersSync,intelSync}.ts` | URL na tushe da ke bai ɗaya ga catalog, referrals, supporter-offers, da ciyarwar Intel waɗanda aka sanya wa hannu daban-daban. Sauya shi don nuna sabis da aka karɓi baƙuncinsa da kai ko aka yi masa fork. |
| `RADAR_FEED_PUBKEY`           | _(maɓallin tsohuwa da aka kafa)_             | `src/lib/radar/pinnedKeys.ts`                                | Maɓallin jama'a na Ed25519 (base64-DER SPKI ko PEM) da ake amfani da shi don tabbatar da sa hannun ciyarwa daga ciyarwa ta musamman.                                                                        |
| `RADAR_CONTRIBUTOR_CLAIM_URL` | `https://radar.omniroute.online/auth/github` | `src/lib/radar/links.ts`                                     | URL da maɓallin dashboard na "Ni mai ba da gudummawa ne" yake buɗewa (hanyar neman supporter-key ta GitHub OAuth).                                                                                          |
| `RADAR_SUPPORTER_PLANS_URL`   | `https://radar.omniroute.online/planos`      | `src/lib/radar/links.ts`                                     | URL da maɓallin dashboard na "Tallafa wa aikin" yake buɗewa (shafin biyan kuɗi/tsare-tsare).                                                                                                                |
| `RADAR_ADMIN_URL`             | _(ba a saita ba)_                            | `src/lib/radar/links.ts`                                     | Keɓaɓɓen mahaɗin kwamitin ayyuka na mai shi kawai. Ana buƙatar HTTPS sai dai don HTTP loopback SSH forward; ƙimomin da ba a saita ba ko marasa inganci ba sa ƙirƙirar abin kewayawa.                        |
