## Bincike: Sauye-sauyen da Aka Cire / Marasa Amfani

Sauye-sauye masu zuwa sun bayyana a sigogin baya na `.env.example` amma **ba su da nassoshin runtime** a cikin codebase na yanzu. An cire su:

| Maɓalli                                                                                                                                                                         | Dalili                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `STORAGE_DRIVER=sqlite`                                                                                                                                                         | Babu wani fayil ɗin tushe da yake karanta shi. SQLite ne kaɗai direban da ake tallafawa — babu buƙatar yin zaɓi.                                                                           |
| `INSTANCE_NAME=omniroute`                                                                                                                                                       | Yana cikin tsoffin takardu/samfuran env amma ba a amfani da shi lokacin aiki. Zai iya dawowa a wata fasalar amfani da instances da yawa a nan gaba.                                        |
| `SQLITE_MAX_SIZE_MB=2048`                                                                                                                                                       | Ba a ambace shi a lambar tushe ba. Ba a sanya iyakar wucin gadi ga girman ma'ajiyar bayanai ba.                                                                                            |
| `SQLITE_CLEAN_LEGACY_FILES=true`                                                                                                                                                | Ba a ambace shi a lambar tushe ba. Mai yiwuwa an cire aikin tsaftace tsoffin fayiloli.                                                                                                     |
| `CLI_ROO_BIN`                                                                                                                                                                   | Ba a yi masa rajista a cikin `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts` ba.                                                                                                                       |
| `CLI_KIMI_CODING_BIN`                                                                                                                                                           | Ba a yi masa rajista a cikin `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts` ba (Kimi Coding yana amfani da OAuth, ba binary na CLI ba).                                                               |
| `IFLOW_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID` / `IFLOW_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`                                                                                                                           | Ba a ambace su a ko'ina cikin lambar tushe ba.                                                                                                                                             |
| `CEREBRAS_API_KEY` / `COHERE_API_KEY` / `FIREWORKS_API_KEY` / `GROQ_API_KEY` / `MISTRAL_API_KEY` / `NEBIUS_API_KEY` / `PERPLEXITY_API_KEY` / `TOGETHER_API_KEY` / `XAI_API_KEY` | An cire su a v3.8.0. Tsarin aiki ya daina karanta waɗannan maɓallan env — bayanan tantancewa suna fitowa daga Dashboard / `data/provider-credentials.json` / ma'ajiyar bayanai mai ɓoyewa. |
| `CURSOR_PROTOBUF_DEBUG`                                                                                                                                                         | An cire shi a v3.8.0. Mai aiwatar da Cursor yana amfani da `CURSOR_DEBUG` / `CURSOR_STREAM_DEBUG` (duba §22).                                                                              |
| `CLI_COMPAT_KIRO`                                                                                                                                                               | An cire shi a v3.8.0. Kiro yana cikin `CLI_COMPAT_OMITTED_PROVIDER_IDS` — maɓallin kunnawa/kashewar sa ba shi da wani tasiri.                                                              |
| `QIANFAN_API_KEY`                                                                                                                                                               | An cire shi tare da sauran samfuran maɓallan API na masu samarwa waɗanda ba a amfani da su a v3.8.0.                                                                                       |

### Gyaran Tsoffin Ƙimomi

| Maɓalli                   | Tsohuwar Ƙimar `.env.example` | Ainihin Tsohuwar Ƙimar Lamba | Gyara                                                                |
| ------------------------- | ----------------------------- | ---------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `APP_LOG_RETENTION_DAYS`  | `90`                          | `7`                          | ✅ An cire ƙimar mai ruɗarwa; an rubuta `7` a matsayin tsohuwar ƙima |
| `CALL_LOG_RETENTION_DAYS` | `90`                          | `7`                          | ✅ An cire ƙimar mai ruɗarwa; an rubuta `7` a matsayin tsohuwar ƙima |

### Sake samar da saitin OpenCode (kayan aikin wucin gadi)

Ana amfani da shi ta `scripts/ad-hoc/regen-opencode-config.ts` don sake samar da `opencode.json`
mai ingantattun ƙimomin `limit.context` da `limit.output` waɗanda aka samo daga instance na
OmniRoute da ke aiki. Ba a buƙatar ko ɗaya daga cikin waɗannan don aiki na yau da kullum — script ɗin
kayan aikin masu haɓakawa ne kawai.

| Sauƙaƙe            | Tsoho                    | Fayil ɗin Tushe                           | Bayani                                                                                                                                                     |
| ------------------ | ------------------------ | ----------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_URL`    | `http://localhost:20128` | `scripts/ad-hoc/regen-opencode-config.ts` | Asalin URL na samfurin OmniRoute da za a tambaya game da `/v1/models`.                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_KEY`    | _(ba a saita ba)_        | `scripts/ad-hoc/regen-opencode-config.ts` | Maɓallin API don tantancewa a ƙarshen OmniRoute na `/v1/models`. Yana komawa ga `OPENCODE_API_KEY` idan ba a saita shi ba.                                 |
| `OPENCODE_API_KEY` | _(ba a saita ba)_        | `scripts/ad-hoc/regen-opencode-config.ts` | Maɓallin API irin na OpenCode (`sk-...`) da ake rubutawa cikin `opencode.json` da aka sake samarwa. Yana komawa ga `OMNIROUTE_KEY` idan ba a saita shi ba. |

### Kayan gwajin compression na offline (kayan aikin ad-hoc)

Ana amfani da shi ta `scripts/compression-eval/index.ts`, wato CLI na kimanta compression na offline.
Ba a buƙatarsa don aiki na yau da kullum — kayan aikin masu haɓakawa ne kawai.

| Sauƙaƙe                      | Tsoho        | Fayil ɗin Tushe                     | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| ---------------------------- | ------------ | ----------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_EVAL_CREDENTIALS` | `{}` (fanko) | `scripts/compression-eval/index.ts` | Shaidun JSON da mai gudanarwa ya bayar don mai samarwar da CLI na compression-eval na offline ke gwadawa (ana fassara su da `JSON.parse`). A bar shi ba a saita ba don gwajin busasshe. |

### Zaman Burauzar VNC

Ana amfani da su ta `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts` don saita zaman Chromium marasa nuni masu amfani da Docker ga masu samar da sarrafa burauza ta atomatik. Duk na zaɓi ne — an nuna tsoffin ƙimomi a ƙasa.

| Sauƙaƙe                               | Tsoho                          | Fayil ɗin Tushe                             | Bayani                                                                                                      |
| ------------------------------------- | ------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_DOCKER_BIN`                | `docker`                       | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Hanyar zuwa binary na Docker da ake amfani da shi don ƙaddamar da kwantenonin VNC.                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IMAGE`                 | `omniroute-vnc-chromium:local` | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Hoton Docker na kwantenan VNC Chromium.                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CHROMIUM_ARGS`         | _(tutocin ginannu)_            | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ƙarin args na Chromium CLI da ake miƙawa burauzar da ke cikin kwantena.                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_VNC_PORT`    | `3000`                         | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Tashar VNC a cikin kwantena.                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_CDP_PORT`    | `9223`                         | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Tashar Chrome DevTools Protocol a cikin kwantena.                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_PROFILE_DIR` | `/config`                      | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Kundin bayanan martaba a cikin kwantena.                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_PROFILE_DIR`           | _(ba a saita ba)_              | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Kundin ɓangaren host don bayanan martabar burauza masu ɗorewa.                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IDLE_MS`               | `600000`                       | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Wa'adin zaman rashin aiki (ms) kafin a girbe zaman VNC.                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_MS`                | `1800000`                      | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Matsakaicin tsawon zama (ms).                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_SESSIONS`          | `4`                            | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Matsakaicin zaman VNC da za su iya gudana a lokaci guda.                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_READY_MS`              | `45000`                        | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Wa'adin jiran shirye-shiryen burauza (ms).                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_HARVEST_MS`            | `20000`                        | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Wa'adin girbi/tsabtacewa (ms).                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_NETWORK`               | `omniroute-vnc-browser-login`  | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Keɓantacciyar hanyar sadarwar Docker da kwantenan ke shiga (#12571), ba tare da amfani da gada ta tsoho ba. |
| `VIBEPROXY_DATA_DIR`                  | _(ba a saita ba)_              | `open-sse/services/notionThreadSessions.ts` | Kundin adana zaman zaren Notion na dindindin.                                                               |

### Tantancewar sabis na ciki

| Sauƙaƙe                                 | Tsoho | Bayani                                                                                                                                   |
| --------------------------------------- | ----- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_INTERNAL_SERVICE_TOKEN`      | –     | Token da aka saka kai tsaye don tantancewar sabis-zuwa-sabis na tsarin gudanarwa.                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_INTERNAL_SERVICE_TOKEN_FILE` | –     | Hanyar zuwa fayil mai ɗauke da token na sabis na ciki (an fi son wannan a cikin kwantena; yana rinjayar sauƙaƙen da aka saka kai tsaye). |

### Ƙididdigar mai samarwar OpenRouter

| Sauƙaƙe                             | Tsoho     | Bayani                                                                                                        |
| ----------------------------------- | --------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OPENROUTER_PROVIDER_STATS_ENABLED` | `true`    | Saita zuwa `false` don tsallake ɗauko ƙididdigar kowane mai samarwa ta OpenRouter don inganta kundin bayanai. |
| `OPENROUTER_PROVIDER_STATS_TTL_MS`  | `3600000` | TTL na ma'ajiyar wucin gadi (ms) don ƙididdigar masu samarwar OpenRouter da aka ɗauko.                        |

### Haɗin Redis da aka saka ciki

| Sauƙaƙe                     | Tsoho       | Bayani                                                                                |
| --------------------------- | ----------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `REDIS_BIND_HOST`           | `127.0.0.1` | Adireshin ɗaurewa na sabis ɗin Redis da aka haɗa a ciki.                              |
| `REDIS_PORT`                | `6379`      | Tashar jiragen ruwa ta sabis ɗin Redis da aka haɗa a ciki.                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_REDIS_BIND_HOST` | –           | Sauyin da aka keɓance ga OmniRoute don adireshin ɗaurewa na Redis da aka haɗa a ciki. |

## 24. Ƙarin abubuwan sakin v3.8.50

An gabatar da waɗannan saitunan bayan hoton yanayin kwangilar mahalli na baya.

| Canji                                       | Tsoho                      | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                                                          | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| ------------------------------------------- | -------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_ADMISSION_QUEUE_MS`         | `2000`                     | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`                                             | Matsakaicin lokacin jiran samun gurbin karɓar tattaunawa mai nauyi kafin a mayar da `503` wanda za a iya sake gwadawa; ɗan gajeren jira mai iyaka yana jera cunkoson wakilai a maimakon mayar da `503` nan take. `0` yana dawo da ƙin amincewa nan take.                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_ADMISSION_MAX_QUEUED_BYTES` | `4194304` (4 MB)           | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`                                             | Kasafin bytes da aka sanya a layin jiran karɓawa: yana iyakance jimillar bytes na body da aka adana a buffer a faɗin dukkan process domin jiran kada ya ƙara nauyin heap (#4380). Jira da ya wuce kasafin yana karɓar `503` mai yiwuwar sake gwadawa nan take.                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_VIRTUAL_TTL_MS`             | `60000` (60 s)             | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`                                             | Tsohon zaɓi marar aiki tun daga #10110: an cire hanyoyin karɓawa na kowane session domin amfani da kasafi guda ɗaya na dukkan process. Ana karɓarsa don dacewar saituna; amma ana watsi da shi.                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_VIRTUAL_MAX_SESSIONS`       | `64`                       | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`                                             | Tsohon zaɓi marar aiki tun daga #10110: an cire hanyoyin karɓawa na kowane session domin amfani da kasafi guda ɗaya na dukkan process. Ana karɓarsa don dacewar saituna; amma ana watsi da shi.                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_VIRTUAL_LANES`              | `0` (a kashe)              | `open-sse/services/admission/runtime.ts`                                                 | Hanyoyin karɓawa na kama-da-wane masu daidaitawa a runtime (#9654): babban maɓalli ne na adaptive gate na kowane tenant (tsari na 2). Ya bambanta da tsofaffin vars na hanyar kowane connection da ke sama (TTL_MS / MAX_SESSIONS, marasa aiki tun daga #10110). Feature flag na Dashboard mai suna iri ɗaya; env var yana rinjayar override na dashboard; yana buƙatar sake farawa. |
| `OMNIROUTE_RUNNOW_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `30000`                    | `src/app/api/jobs/[id]/run-now/route.ts`                                                 | Yana iyakance tsawon lokacin da kiran run-now zai jira job da ke gudana kafin ya fara run ɗin da aka sanya a layi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BROWSER_REFRESH`             | a kunne                    | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                                               | Yana sabunta IMS da yanayin haɗarin browser ta hanyar Chrome CDP sessions na kowane account; saita `0` don kashewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_SESSION_DISK`                | a kunne                    | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                                               | Yana adana Adobe sessions da aka gyara ƙarƙashin `DATA_DIR`; saita `0` don yanayin memory kawai.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_MIN_SUBMIT_GAP_MS`           | `12000`                    | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                                               | Mafi ƙarancin tazara tsakanin aika buƙatun generate na Adobe Firefly.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BATCH_EXTRA_GAP_MS`          | `15000`                    | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                                               | Ƙarin lokacin hutu bayan kowane aika buƙatar Adobe na uku da ya yi nasara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_CHROME_HEADLESS`             | `0`                        | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflyBrowserLogin.ts`                                          | Yanayin true-headless na debugging kawai; Adobe colligo yawanci yana ƙin risk session da hakan ke samarwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `CHROME_PATH`                               | ganowa ta atomatik         | `open-sse/executors/cloudflare-playground.ts`, `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts` | Cikakken path na Chrome executable na zaɓi wanda executors masu amfani da browser ke amfani da shi idan ganowar platform ta atomatik ba ta isa ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN`                        | _(ba a saita ba)_          | `src/lib/telegram/config.ts`                                                             | Token na BotFather wanda ke kunna webhook mai shigowa kuma yake sanya hannu kan Mini App `initData`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `TELEGRAM_WEBHOOK_SECRET`                   | _(ba a saita ba)_          | `src/lib/telegram/config.ts`                                                             | Shared secret da aka yi wa rajista ta `setWebhook` kuma ake tabbatar da shi da header na `X-Telegram-Bot-Api-Secret-Token` a duk lokacin isar da webhook. Ana buƙatarsa don path na webhook; idan ba a saita shi ba, ana ƙin isar da webhook da 503.                                                                                                                                 |
| `TELEGRAM_DEFAULT_MODEL`                    | `auto/chat`                | `src/lib/telegram/chatProxy.ts`                                                          | Model da ake amfani da shi don amsoshin tattaunawar Telegram.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `TELEGRAM_BOT_API_BASE`                     | `https://api.telegram.org` | `src/lib/telegram/config.ts`                                                             | Override na asalin URL na Bot API don proxies ko sabar Bot API da aka saukar da kansu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `TELEGRAM_WEBHOOK_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `60000`                    | `src/lib/telegram/config.ts`                                                             | Timeout a cikin milliseconds don kiran Bot API masu fita.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_OPTIONAL_PACK_TAR`               | `1` (a kunne)              | `scripts/build/optionalPackStaging.mjs`                                                  | Saita `0` don tsallake fitar da tarballs na `.tar.gz` yayin shirya optional ML/browser packs don bishiyar Electron standalone (har yanzu ana samar da pack directories da `optional-packs.index.json`). Desktop release workflow ne ke amfani da shi don rage girman artifact da ake lodawa.                                                                                         |

### ChatGPT Web (Codex)

Saitunan tsoho na duniya don headless browser da tool-tunnel mai fita. Ƙimomin Connection da aka saita a Dashboard suna da fifiko.

| Sauyawa                                 | Tsoho                          | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                                            | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| --------------------------------------- | ------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_CHROME_PATH`         | _(ganowa ta atomatik)_         | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | Takamaiman hanyar Chrome/Chromium don gudanarwa ta npm, systemd da PM2.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `CHROME_PATH`                           | _(ganowa ta atomatik)_         | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | Madadin gama-gari don takamaiman hanyar Chrome/Chromium.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_CDP_URL`             | _(ba a saita ba)_              | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | Ƙarshen CDP na ciki; Docker yana amfani da sidecar a tashar `9223`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `CDP_PROXY_TOKEN`                       | _(ba a saita ba)_              | `docker/chatgpt-web-codex-browser/cdp-proxy.mjs`                           | Idan an saita shi, dole ne kowace buƙata zuwa sidecar na wakilin CDP ta aika wannan ƙima a cikin header `X-Omni-Cdp-Token` (#13679). Idan babu ƙima, wakilin zai tura buƙatun ba tare da tantancewa ba — a wannan yanayin, keɓewar hanyar sadarwar Compose `chatgpt-web-codex-net` ce kawai ke ba da kariya. Ƙirƙira da `openssl rand -hex 32`. |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_TUNNEL_ID`           | _(ba a saita ba)_              | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | ID na tunnel na OpenAI na bai ɗaya don zagayen kayan aikin Codex na gida.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_RUNTIME_KEY`         | _(ba a saita ba)_              | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | Runtime key na tunnel na bai ɗaya; kada a taɓa fitar da shi cikin logs.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_CONNECTOR_NAME`      | `OmniRoute Codex v2`           | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | Ainihin sunan sabon ChatGPT Custom Connector da aka ƙirƙira don gadar MCP.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_HOME`                | `<DATA_DIR>/chatgpt-web-codex` | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/config.ts`                              | Keɓaɓɓen kundin adireshi don yanayin browser, broker da tunnel.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_BROWSER_DIAGNOSTICS` | `0`                            | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/adapters/chatgpt-web/browser-worker.ts` | Idan ƙimar ita ce `1`, ana ɗaukar hotunan binciken matsalar browser a kowane checkpoint.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_LAUNCHER`            | _(ba a saita ba)_              | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/config.ts`                              | Hanyar cikakke ta zaɓi zuwa launcher binary na dindindin.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_BUN`                 | _(ganowa ta atomatik)_         | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/config.ts`                              | Hanyar cikakke ta zaɓi zuwa Bun runtime binary.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `CODEX_WEB_GPT_BUN`                     | _(ba a saita ba)_              | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/config.ts`                              | Tsohon madadi don `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_BUN`; sabbin saituna suna amfani da sunan da aka amince da shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

## Gadar OmniConductor

Mai karɓar SSE mai dogon rai wanda ke kwafin ayyukan cibiyar OmniConductor zuwa cikin A2A TaskManager na gida (`src/lib/conductor/`). Na zaɓi ne — gadar tana farawa ne kawai idan an saita `CONDUCTOR_HUB_URL`. Na ɓangaren sabar kawai: kada alamar tantancewar cibiyar ta taɓa isa burauza.

| Sauyi                          | Tsoho                   | Fayil ɗin Tushe                  | Bayani                                                                                                                                     |
| ------------------------------ | ----------------------- | -------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `CONDUCTOR_HUB_URL`            | _(fanko)_               | `src/lib/conductor/boot.ts`      | URL na asalin cibiyar OmniConductor (misali `http://127.0.0.1:7910`). Ba a saita ba = an kashe gada.                                       |
| `CONDUCTOR_HUB_TOKEN`          | _(fanko)_               | `src/lib/conductor/boot.ts`      | Bayanan tantancewar cibiyar don rafin SSE — ƙirƙiri takwaran mai nau'in `spokesperson` a cibiyar (`POST /v1/peers`, admin).                |
| `CONDUCTOR_ORCHESTRATOR_TOKEN` | _(fanko)_               | `src/lib/conductor/hubProxy.ts`  | Bayanan tantancewa don miƙa ayyukan A2A→cibiya masu shigowa (`POST /v1/tasks`); yana koma wa `CONDUCTOR_HUB_TOKEN` idan ba a saita shi ba. |
| `CONDUCTOR_SPOKESPERSON_URL`   | `http://127.0.0.1:7920` | `src/lib/conductor/faroProxy.ts` | URL na asalin sabis ɗin mai magana da yawun jama'a (Faro) da ke bayan wakilin taɗi na dashboard (`/api/conductor/ask`).                    |

### Tsarawa mai la'akari da ƙayyadadden amfani

`open-sse/services/combo.ts` da `src/lib/quota/quotaScheduler.ts` suna amfani da shi don binciken kasafin token kafin buƙata. Na zaɓi ne — tsarin turawa na tsoho ba ya canzawa idan ba a saita shi ba.

| Sauyi                           | Tsoho | Fayil ɗin Tushe              | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ------------------------------- | ----- | ---------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_QUOTA_AWARE_ROUTING` | `0`   | `open-sse/services/combo.ts` | Idan `1`, a tsallake haɗin da kasafin token ɗinsu na kowane zangon lokaci (`rateLimitOverrides.tpm`, teburin `provider_quota_state`) ba zai iya ɗaukar kiyasin kuɗin buƙatar ba kafin aikawa. A bari a ci gaba idan ba a saita kasafi ba. |
